# Agent log

Newest entry on top. One entry per run: date, chunk, what changed, commit,
what is next, defaults applied.

Older entries are in [`log/`](log/): [1–19 November 2026](log/2026-11-01-to-19.md) and [October 2026](log/2026-10.md).

## 2026-11-26 · chat · measuring a business of a few hundred people, part 1a: the test business, made locally

- On chat/scale-300 from origin/main 21a80d42 (#274). The measuring plan's PR 1a (Orel's decision of 22.11 on #1; readiness review section 4 item 11, then item 9; ROADMAP 82): the test business, its generator and its database test, with no browser, no workflow and no Playwright project that loads a page. No migration, no new npm package, no change to `supabase/seed.sql`, `hosted-demo.sql`, `e2e/`, `playwright.config.ts` or the six e2e shards. No customer is named anywhere: "a business of a few hundred people".
- **The shape**, `measure/scale/shape.ts` and `ids.ts` (pure, typed): `shape({ people, depth, months, chat, anchor })` plans every row before anything is written, for 30, 100, 300 and 600 people. "עסק בדיקה" (`scale-test`, id `5ca1e300-…-b000-000000000300`) on the `general` template, not a demo; at 300 an owner, 4 managers, 10 shift leads (the named role "אחראי/ת משמרת" holding clock.edit), 285 workers, 60 who left, an accountant and the console's platform admin; 10 departments (one of about 100, nine of about 22, 30 people in two); 30 patterns, 200 shifts a week round the clock (20 of 7 days, 10 nights of 6, staggered an hour across departments so some shift always runs two hours more), the big department's morning needing 50; a fixed line a person, so two of anyone's shifts are a day apart. 13 months: 59 weeks, 11,800 shifts, 65,000 assignments, 60,000 entries, 48,000 breaks; 600 entries without a code and 20 of the managers' own waiting now, 15 on the clock, 100 punchers with no shift today; 2,000 messages in the team group, 300 in each department, 150 private chats, 20 announcements with 4,400 acks; a procedure to everyone and four months of payslips (1,107 documents, 1,405 recipients); 1,436 wage rows; 130 invites. And "עסק שכן" (`scale-neighbour`), 7 people with the same rates of hours, log and lines. Every choice is md5(SEED_VERSION, kind, subject, day), its first 8 hex digits over 2³²; dates are relative to the anchor in Asia/Jerusalem (`instantIn`/`dateIn` from `@domain/availability`). Names "<first> בדיקה <NNN>" ("נועם בדיקה 017"), every 25th a long double one of one form ("יוסף-אליעזר בדיקה-ארוכה 025"), a third with a business name ("סמאח ב׳ 018", never the owner's); logins `p<6 digits>@scale.conceptapp.invalid`, no phone; display numbers `+1 2NN 555 01xx` (201 to 210 for people, 211 and 212 for invites); the import's block 050-000-6100 to 6399, checked free. Settings are the modules' own manifests' parse (`@modules/scheduling/manifest`, `@modules/time_clock/manifest`). It refuses an anchor within 2 hours of a week's or a month's start.
- **The seed**, `measure/scale/seed.ts`, `parts.ts` and `sql/00-guard.sql` … `99-counts.sql`: TypeScript plans the rows, and set-based SQL parts write them, one statement after each `-- step:` line, its rows in `$1` as JSON (2,000 at a time), the run's settings by `current_setting('scale.…')`, no dollar quote and no DO block. A failure names the part, the step and the line (Postgres's position turned into the file's line), with the code, detail, hint and where; a step returning a `problem` column fails with each one. After every part the counts read back (99-counts, two `jsonb_build_object`s since a call takes at most 100 arguments) must equal the shape's, or it stops listing each that differs. In order: the guard (dev.send_sms and dev.sms_codes there, no `app_url` or `cron_secret` in Vault, the eight cron jobs off, no demo row, no device of the prefix), clean first, logins and profiles, the businesses through `apply_template` with settings, departments (their groups open), the owner and the named role through `create_business_role` and `set_role_permission`, memberships backdated (staff_since and the 'joined' period follow `created_at`), the business names after them, departments' members backdated, leavers inactive with their periods written, wages, payroll details, invites; every week's row with the seed's id, `open_schedule_week` finding the three the app writes, history written directly, last week and this week through `publish_week_schedule` as the scheduling manager (this week's first publish tells only those with a shift still to start), next week through `save_week_schedule` with the 50-person shift; availability, swaps (20 open on this week's shifts ahead, 150 declined or withdrawn, 200 never answered on past shifts), hours, breaks, `record_absence` as the owner; chat, documents (each with its `private.file_uploads` and `storage.objects` row), the bell through `private.notify` (who joined, to team.view; the last 25 weeks of history published), seven in ten of them read by the md5 choice computed in SQL; the history's log in one insert ordered by `created_at` (clock.in, clock.out, clock.confirm, clock.correct with `public.correct_time_entry`'s details, two break rows a break, payroll.view on each working day), checked to have written no line; then the waiting entries' clock.in and clock.out, whose trigger writes exactly the clock.confirm lines the shape counts (one an entry for each clock.edit holder but its person; the owners for a manager's own). `analyze` at the end for the measuring run. `measure/scale/clean.ts`: Storage's rows (with `storage.allow_delete_query`, an optional remover for real files for PR 3a), `private.purge_business_records`, the businesses, the logins; then 0 rows of the prefix in nine tables. `cost.ts`: the database MB a month (each growing table's share by the business's rows, over the months), Storage at 150 KB a payslip and months until 50 and 200 MB, the chat's files at a stated rate, labelled arithmetic.
- **Entry points**: `pnpm measure:seed` and `pnpm measure:clean` (`measure/seed.config.ts`, two Playwright projects of one file each, no browser, no web server; MEASURE_PEOPLE and MEASURE_MONTHS), the seed's counts, times and cost to `measure/out/seed-<people>.json` (git-ignored). `scale/guard.ts` refuses any database but loopback on 54322, `VERCEL` set, or a non-loopback `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL`; `refuseUnlessTestLogin` for 1b's sessions.
- **CI**: `vitest.config.mts` takes `measure/**/*.test.ts`; `measure/scale/scale.db.test.ts` runs in the database job's `pnpm test:db`, its step renamed "Database tests (vitest)" (still never naming measure). Biome's `measure/**` override (Playwright's lint domain; `@/…`, `@ui`, `@db` and module internals refused) and `tooling/boundaries.test.ts` (its probes for measure/, and nothing in app/, packages/ or modules/ importing measure/). CLAUDE.md's Layout and Commands, `measure/README.md`, `docs/measure.md` (first part, Hebrew), ROADMAP 82.
- Tests. Unit (`pnpm check`): `measure/scale/shape.test.ts` (25: each size's team, departments, patterns and shifts a week; every cap; the 50 at 300 and the draft filling it; the departments at 300; the settings through the manifests; 51 people refused by `scheduledShift`, 50 taken; nobody on two shifts under 8 hours apart or more than 5 a week, at 30 and at 300 over 13 months; entries never overlapping, nothing after the anchor, breaks inside; the 15 open entries in shifts with 2 hours or more; the 100 punchers; the proofs' mix, 600 and 20 waiting, the self ones the managers', the confirm lines' count; the database job's 30 with 2 months; the anchor's refusals; the same hash for the same anchor and another for another day; fake names, fictional and unique numbers, no `+9725` in the shape, the import block, every id with the prefix), `guard.test.ts` (5), `parts.test.ts` (6: the files in order, single statements, every step given its rows and only those, the columns of each `as r (…)` equal to the keys sent, an error's part, step and line, the SQL choice), `tooling/measure-guards.test.ts` (7: the prefix, the fictional numbers and the import block nowhere outside measure/ and docs/; supabase/ and migrate-*.yml never mention measure/; ci.yml never says measure; no measuring file in e2e/, `testDir: "e2e"`, the seed config with no web server, the two scripts; `[auth] enable_signup = false` and the send_sms hook at dev.send_sms; a measure.yml, once there, reading only with no secret), `tooling/boundaries.test.ts` (6 probes and 1 test more). Database (`pnpm test:db`): 30 people with 2 months and 300 at people depth, each in its own transaction rolled back, with the per-part counts above, then: the md5 vector in SQL against TypeScript; `session_replication_role` origin and every app trigger on the written tables on; a member of two years reads the team group's oldest message through `chat_page` as themself, the newest joiner does not; leavers' periods all past; `member_names` the shape's, none the owner's; only clock.confirm, schedule.published and team.joined lines; the neighbour's owner under RLS reads 0 of the big business's entries, log, lines and memberships and some of their own; at 300 the draft's 200 shifts with 50 on the biggest, 201 refused with "Too many shifts", 51 on a shift needing 50 printed; publishing next week 299 schedule.published lines, none the accountant's, none for a phone, "unfilled" printed; a worker's message in the team group 299 lines; a document to everyone (reserved, put in place, sent) 299 recipients and 299 lines; an import of 300 at the block, 300 invites and 400 waiting; the never-send checks; removal to 0 rows; times printed and to the step summary, a warning past 30 s, a failure past 60 s.
- **Settled in this PR** (the plan's four): the named-role function is `public.create_business_role(owner, business, 'worker', name)` then `public.set_role_permission(owner, business, 'worker', 'clock.edit', true, role)`, before any member holds it, so it tells nobody. `clock.correct`'s details are not `{entry, from}` but `public.correct_time_entry`'s `{entry, person, from: {clock_in, clock_out}, to: {clock_in, clock_out}, proof: {in, out}, note}`, with `closed_month` only in a closed month, which history never is. The md5 vector: `('x' || substr(md5(version || ':' || kind || ':' || subject || ':' || day), 1, 8))::bit(32)::bigint`, unsigned, over 2³² in TypeScript, checked on 40 inputs in the database test and used for real in the read marks. Durations against main's: on #274's and the two runs before it the database job took 1m43s to 2m07s (its vitest step 3 to 4 s) and the check job 4m to 4m41s, so the database job ends some 2.5 minutes before check, which e2e also waits for; the added seconds (a target of 15 a size) don't move e2e unless they pass about 150, and the fallback job isn't needed. This PR's own run gives the real numbers.
- **The plan against the code** (followed the code): with `requirePunchCode` at its default, off, every punch reads 'off' (`private.check_punch`), so nothing waits and no punch is 'scan' or 'none': the test business switches it on, which is what its mix and its 600 waiting need. There is no monthly wage for a member (`member_wages` holds an hourly rate alone; `monthly_agorot` is the minimum wage's): all are hourly. Publishing never writes `schedule.unfilled` lines; it lowers or settles those there (`private.notify_for_team`, 20261115000900). A live swap on a draft is not a thing the app makes, so the 20 live ones are on this week's shifts ahead, and in the week's last three hours there are fewer (as many as the places left on shifts starting more than an hour on). 1,311 placed a week at 300, not 1,500, so about 100 people are off on most days for the punch queue (a third work 5 shifts, the rest 4; days off laid end to end so each day has about as many off as another); when fewer are off (some days have about 95, and past midnight last night's shifts still run) the rest of the 100 are taken off today's shifts before the hours are planned. The plan's 15 on the clock in shifts with 2 hours or more left can't all be had at 300 around 06:00 and 22:00, when most shifts end within the hour: the rest come from the running shifts with the most time left (at 30 and 100 nobody is on the clock while the business is closed). The work counts the plan has the database job print at 30 and 300 (rows examined, lines, helper calls) are not printed: at 300 the job seeds people depth, with no hours or log for the reads to examine, so they move to the measuring run, which seeds 300 with its history; the job prints the counts, the slowest steps and the lines each write at 300 tells. 13 on the biggest shift at 30, not 6: 126 placements over 17 shifts don't fit 6 a shift. Nights round the clock skip a day each (the plan's 10 patterns of 6 days); closed on Shabbat, all of a business's shifts keep the same hours, since a night from midnight would fall on Shabbat. History scans carry no station (none exists until 1b pairs one; `in_station` is nullable and set null on a station's delete). Cancelled history entries get no clock.cancel row (it only settles lines; the plan's list of actions has none). The plan's "the 50-person shift" in the draft is read as filled to its 50.
- Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check` (5477 tests), every SQL file split and matched to the rows sent by `parts.test.ts`, each statement read against the migrations' tables, constraints, triggers and functions it touches. Not run here: no Docker on this machine, so the SQL has never run; CI's database job is its first run (the seed stops naming the part, the step and the line), and `pnpm measure:seed` at 300 with 13 months waits for a machine with Docker or PR 1b's run. Not done: the push and the pull request.
- Defaults applied, for Orel: `requirePunchCode` on in the test business (else nothing waits); all wages hourly; 1,311 placed a week at 300, the punchers made up to 100 from today's shifts when fewer are off; the live swaps on this week only, fewer in its last three hours; on the clock, the shifts with the most time left when too few have 2 hours; the work counts with the measuring run, not the database job; seed docs in Hebrew (`docs/measure.md`) with the developers' notes in English (`measure/README.md`); docs/ may describe the prefix and the numbers (the guard scans code and config).
- After review (seven findings, all real; two were the same one): **the rows went to SQL encoded twice**: `runPart` sent `JSON.stringify(chunk)`, `$1::jsonb` made postgres.js take the parameter as jsonb and stringify it again, so the seed would have stopped at 10-people's first step ("cannot call jsonb_to_recordset on a scalar") and the database job gone red; now `tx.json(chunk)`, and `tx.json(vector)` for the md5 vector in the database test, the SQL unchanged; `parts.test.ts` checks the rows go as a typed JSON parameter, chunked, and that nothing in measure/ casts a JSON string to jsonb. **The cost a month** divided every table by the history's months, though the chat covers 90 days and the documents their months of payslips: `spansOf` gives each table the months its rows cover in the shape (the hours, breaks and log from the history's first week; shifts and assignments over their weeks; the chat over its 90 days; documents and recipients over the payslip months, the procedure with them), each row and the label naming what it was divided by; the bell's lines are out of the MB a month, given as what the app would hold under its cleaning (180 days, read ones 90), the waiting hours' clock.confirm backlog left out (`linesHeld`); `cost.test.ts` (2). **The fixed counts held only at the test's anchor**: on the clock now takes whoever has arrived (10 minutes early to 5 late) in a shift still running, two hours or more left first, then the most time left; the punchers are taken first, the ones off today by choice and the rest off today's shifts, before the hours; live swaps stay on this week, fewer in its last three hours, said in the docs and LOG. Swept here every 15 minutes through a week: at 300, 15 on the clock and 100 punchers at every anchor the run takes (as few as 6 and 89 before), at 600 30 and 200, at 30 and 100 the punchers always and nobody on the clock while closed; `shape.test.ts` sweeps 48 anchors at 300 (four-hourly, and the hours that fell short) and fails on the old code. **The history's log came after the anchor's own rows**: 40-weeks is now 92-weeks and `record_absence` its own 93-absences, both after 90-log (nothing before needs the weeks: no entry ties to a shift, and the bell's history of publishing names its weeks in its lines alone), the expectations moved with them, and the database test checks every clock.confirm and clock.correct id is below every schedule.publish and clock.absence_add id; the named role's two rows still come first, as a business's first. **The work counts** move to the measuring run, said in the plan against the code. **The example names** didn't exist: the docs and the LOG take theirs from `nameOf` ("נועם בדיקה 017", "סמאח ב׳ 018", "יוסף-אליעזר בדיקה-ארוכה 025"), and a double name's second is of the first's form (`SECOND_NAMES` by form, with a test). Checked: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check` (5483 tests). Still not run here: the SQL (no Docker).

## 2026-11-26 · chat · inviting from a list (הזמנה מרשימה), part 6: the wizard reads lists as the import does

- On chat/wizard-lists from origin/main 703b4ea3 (#273; parts 1–5 on main). The plan's PR 6 of section 7 ("אשף העסק החדש קורא רשימות כמו הייבוא"): the console's new-business wizard reads its staff step with part 1's reader, and asks where else a number works with the numbers in the request's body. One migration, no new npm package, no new `packages/ui` component (so no /design gallery entry). Nothing of PR 7's or later: no department, no file.
- **The reader**, `@domain/business`: `parseStaffList(input, reading)` now lives here (it imports `@domain/people-import`, which imports `@domain/staff-list`, so it can't stay there) and reads with `parsePeopleList` against `staffRolesOf(labels)`, `importRolesOf` over `inviteChoices` for an owner (every role, none blocked). So a header row and a title above it, an ID or wage column (left out and named), direction marks, Excel's dropped zero in a phone column, two phones on a line, Excel's "5.01E+08", a reversed line, a name line and its number line, `parseTeamName` (code points, NFC, what nobody sees) and a role at either end of a line work there as in the import. Owners and accountants stay the wizard's to invite: the import holds a line naming one for one by one, so `@domain/people-import` now carries which on the problem line (`role?: "owner" | "accountant"`, from the matcher through `roleRead` and `lineOf`; nothing else reads it), and the wizard takes it as that person. The same number twice is one person by the same name (case aside) and role, an owner's line among them (`sameLine`, left out, no longer a problem), else `duplicate` naming the first line with the number. `labels` are the app's words for the roles (`app.roles` in the viewer's language, `roleWordsOf`), so "ניהול" and "צוות" read as the import reads a business's own; the form and the action pass the same. `elsewhere` holds the numbers the database found on another staff: a line inviting one of them to the staff (not as an accountant) is to fix, `elsewhere`, until it goes. The wizard keeps its 60 (`STAFF_LIST_MAX`, still in `staff-list.ts` with `StaffEntry` and the role words); past the import's 300 people or 200,000 characters it is `tooMany` with no lines drawn; two columns of mobiles are `phoneColumns`, a new `NewBusinessErrors.staff`. `parseNewBusiness(input, only, reading)` passes `reading` on. `staff-list.ts` loses its old reader, its separators and list marker.
- **The screen**, `app/(admin)/admin/new/new-business-form.tsx`: the preview says each line's problem in the import's words (`useLineWords(roles, "console")` from `app/(app)/team/import/import-lines.tsx`: "שורה 3 · המספר לא נראה כמו נייד ישראלי", the duplicate's line, "המספר כבר בצוות של עסק אחר"), with a warning mark and the line as pasted; the header row read ("שורה 1 נקראה כשורת הכותרות.") and the columns left out (`ignoredWords`); each person's notes and what of their line wasn't read ("לא נקלט: 123456782"); and the lines left out (a title, the same line again) with why. Its live line says too many or two phone columns when the list is that. The field's error for two phone columns is the import's `status.phoneColumns` (or `phoneColumnsHeaded`); a refusal for numbers on another staff still names them in the field, and now also marks their lines, from the action's `elsewhere`. The hint says a sheet with its header row works (`admin.newBusiness.staff.hint`, he and en); `staff.line` and `staff.lineErrors` go, since the import's words say it now. Words added: `admin.peopleImport.problem.beforeHeader`, he and en (after review, below).

- **The database**, `20261128000400_staff_elsewhere.sql` (no branch on origin uses the number; main's latest was …000300): `public.admin_staff_elsewhere(actor uuid, phones text[], except_business uuid default null) returns text[]` (the third argument and its second caller, `activeElsewhere`, with known issue 109, below), plpgsql, stable, security definer, `search_path = ''`: platform staff only (42501, as every `admin_` function asks), at most 300 numbers (54000, hint `limit`), and of the numbers given those an active member holds on a staff, an accountant's place aside (`private.is_staff_role`, the rule of `memberships_one_staff_idx`), each once in the order given, `'{}'` for none, an empty list or no list. A demo business's staff count as any other's (their memberships hold the same index). It reads only: no invite, no audit row, no notification, and no refusal holds a number. Revoked from public, anon and authenticated, granted to service_role. Two dollar quotes, counted after writing. `database.types.ts` has its entry in the generator's shape (`Args` on one line, the entry expanded since it passes 80 columns; `tooling/database-types.test.ts` green). `onAnotherStaff(actorId, entries)` in `@db/invites` calls it with the list's numbers in a staff role, each once, as a POST body (supabase-js's `rpc`, no `get`), Zod-parses the answer and gives it in the list's order; before the function reaches a project (`isMissingFunction`) it finds none and asks no other way: no GET fallback with the numbers in an address. - Checked against the code at 703b4ea3, where it differs from the plan or the task: (1) The plan's `parsePeopleList` holds owners and accountants for one by one and said nothing of how the wizard keeps them: the problem line now carries the role named, which the import never reads. ## 2026-11-26 · chat · inviting from a list (הזמנה מרשימה), part 5: the Team screen

- On chat/import-team from 568a1ba8, the head of chat/import-console (part 4; it held main fce2fea with parts 1–3). Part 4 has since merged as #272 (7bb37a92, the same tree as 568a1ba8), and main is merged here, so the pull request shows part 5 alone. The plan's PR 5 of section 7 ("הזמנה מרשימה במסך הצוות"), under Orel's decisions of 25.11.2026: owners and managers paste on /team, after the console; each person's link is made at the moment of sending ("יצירת קישור", then WhatsApp straight to the number, or a copy); the app sends nothing. This completes the MVP (parts 1–5). No migration, no SQL, no new npm package, no new `packages/ui` component (so no /design gallery entry): the send list is part 2's `SendLinkRow` and its lists. Nothing of PR 6's or later: the wizard still reads its own staff list, no department, no file.
- **The route**, `app/(app)/team/import/page.tsx`: not found unless the person may invite one at a time, exactly as /team/invite asks (`invitableRoles`, then `invitesOpen`, so never a hosted demo, #80), and unless a staff role is left them to give (`listRoleOf` over the roles below): owners and accountants are invited one at a time. The roles are the invite form's (`inviteChoices` with `currentOwnerOnlyRoles` and `currentGivableNamedRoles`, those only an owner gives shown with why), worded as the business words its roles (`translator(currentWording(), "app.roles")`); the team is the Team screen's own (`currentTeam`, under RLS) through the new `teamKnownOf`. The view is part 1's `PeopleImportView` in team mode, keyed by person and business as /team is, with the bound actions, the way back to /team, and the send list's actions (`firstLinkAction`, `resendInviteAction`, the business's name, WhatsApp to the number). The title goes through `translator`, never next-intl's server translator.
- **The actions**, `app/(app)/team/import/actions.ts`: `importPeopleAction(business, input)` and `undoPeopleImportAction(business, importId)`, bound on the page to the business it was drawn for. In `changer`'s order with the invite form's words: no member or no team.edit `noPermission`; a bound id that is no id `changed`; another business `businessChanged`; a session viewing as someone `viewOnly`. Then Zod at the boundary (`peopleImportRows`: `changed`, nothing asked, nothing logged), then each row as the invite form would take it from this person (`parseTeamInvite` against the live named roles, the same name, number, role and named role back; a named role gone `roleGone`; a built-in role only an owner gives, which `parseTeamInvite` doesn't check, `changed`), then `importPeopleToTeam`. Its refusals keep their words (`tooManyOpen` with `open`, `demo`, `notAllowed`, `givesPay`, `roleGone`, `tooMany`, `changed`, `failed`); `elsewhere`, which the Team screen's function never answers, would be `failed`, never said (#80). Done: `revalidatePath("/team")` and `refresh()`, as part 4's, and the answer rebuilt key by key, so no link or token rides along. A throw is `failed`, its message alone logged. The undo: `isUuid`, `undoTeamImport`, `{ok, cancelled, kept}`.
- **The done screen's send list** (team mode only, `send` on the view): `done-links.ts` (`doneLinksOf`, no React) lists, from the database's answer, every row it made, updated or found the same with its invite, in the role it ends with (`writtenRoleOf`, else the invite the screen read, else none said), and every line the screen itself left out as already waiting with no link that the viewer may change (the wizard's manager pasted with no role), once each; and, apart, each line whose invite's link ran out that the viewer may renew. `done-send.tsx`: `DoneLinks` puts part 2's `InviteLinks` and `PendingLinks` around the whole view, so a list taken back and kept again with its "ביטול" still shows the links made, never "יצירת קישור" again; `DoneSendList` is /team's own rows (`PendingList` on a phone and a tablet, `PendingTableRows` in a table on a computer, each with `data-pending-links`), so it is the same flow as #266: "יצירת קישור", WhatsApp to the number or the copy, the row says which, and the focus goes on to the next person in the shape on screen; `DoneExpired` folds those whose link ran out into one row ("הקישור הקודם פג", "אדם אחד מהרשימה", show and hide), each with part 2's `ResendLink` ("קישור חדש"), `app.team.invites.expiredHint` under them. Layout: one under the other on a phone; on a tablet the done card at the start, the list at the end; on a computer the card above and the table across under it (side by side, the link column wouldn't fit). The done screen has no accent button: every "יצירת קישור" is secondary, as on /team. A row whose role the screen can't know (an invite made meanwhile elsewhere) says none, and /team's list drops its " · " then.
- **The entry rows.** `TeamView` takes `importHref`, drawn only where invites are, for whoever edits: a quiet `ListGroup`/`ListRow` with `ListPlus`, "הזמנה מרשימה · יש רשימה? כמה אנשים בבת אחת", at the foot of the invites on a phone and a tablet and under the table on a computer (hidden with the invites under a file filter's choice). `TeamInviteView` takes it too, above the form. Both pages pass it only when the import would open for the viewer (`importsPeople`, the new rule, and `invitesOpen`), so no row leads to a page not found; "הזמנה לצוות" stays the screen's one main action.
- **@domain/people-import.** `importsPeople(choices)`: a staff role among the invite form's choices that isn't an owner's alone. `teamKnownOf(team, viewer, named, ownerOnly)`: the Team screen's `KnownTeam`, an invite `touchable` only when the viewer would make its link now (a manager's or a worker's, `canResendInvite`, and a role that sees pay only for an owner), as /team's `resendable` offers it.
- **Words** (he and en, same keys and placeholders): `app.team.import.entry`, `entryHint`, `done.expired.title`, `done.expired.count`. The send list's are part 2's `app.team.pending.*` and `app.team.link.*`.
- **The twin's send list**, which #266 left for this PR: `/design/shell/team/import?state=done` now shows it under the done card, with the Team twin's stand-in for each link (`demoResendAction`) and WhatsApp to nobody (`toNumber: false`, so it asks whom to send to), since a list pasted on the hosted twin may be real. The stand-in hears only the twin's invite id. The twin's invites now have ids of their own across imports, kept as they are updated (they were the row's place, so a second list's first person showed the first list's link). The Team twin and the invite twin draw the entry rows (the invite twin's `?as=paid` none, with no role left).

## 2026-11-26 · chat · inviting from a list (הזמנה מרשימה), part 4: the console

- On chat/import-console from 13487fcc, the head of chat/import-db (part 3, since squash-merged as #271, fce2feaa, on #270, and main merged in here after the check; it held main f84bb68, with parts 1 and 2 and #269). The plan's PR 4 of section 7 ("הוספת אנשים מרשימה בקונסולה"), under Orel's decision of 25.11.2026 that the ConceptApp team pastes first, in the console, at setup: the console's route, its actions and their tests, the console's words, the links to it, the card for a demo, the done card with its undo, the console twin and the e2e. No migration, no SQL, no new npm package, no new `packages/ui` component (so no /design gallery entry). Nothing of PR 5's: no `/team/import`, no send list, no entry rows on /team or /team/invite.
- **The route**, `app/(admin)/admin/businesses/[slug]/people/import/`: `page.tsx` (notFound without `staff.invite` or for an unknown slug; a business closed to invites, `invitesOpen`, gets `ConsoleImportClosed` from `closed-view.tsx`: the console's title, an `EmptyState` "לעסק הזה לא מזמינים אנשים" with `admin.setup.invite.errors.demo` and the way back, no form; else `consoleKnownTeam`, `getGivableNamedRoles` and the business's words for the built-in roles, read with `getWordingForTeam` and `wordingFrom` + `translator(…, "app.roles")`, so the team pastes the words the business uses), `console-roles.ts` (`CONSOLE_GIVER`, an owner with no perms, and `consoleImportRoles`: `importRolesOf` over `inviteChoices` for that giver, managers, workers and the named roles on them, none blocked), and `actions.ts`: `importConsolePeopleAction({id, slug}, input)` and `undoConsoleImportAction({id, slug}, importId)`. Both check `platformCan(staff.role, "staff.invite")` (`noPermission`), then Zod at the boundary: the bound business as a strict `{id, slug}` (an id `isUuid` takes, the slug rule; else `changed`), the rows with `peopleImportRows` (`changed`, nothing asked, nothing logged), then each row as "הוספת אדם" would take it (`parseTeamInvite` for an owner against the live named roles, the same name, number, role and named role back; a named role no longer live is `roleGone`, anything else `changed`), then `importPeopleFromConsole`, whose refusals keep their words (`elsewhere` with the rows' places, `tooManyOpen` with `open` when the database gave it, `demo`, `roleGone`, `tooMany`, `changed`, `failed`); a throw is `failed`, its message alone logged. Done, `revalidatePath` of the business page and `refresh()`, and the answer is `{ok, importId, rows: [{i, status, invite}]}`, rebuilt key by key, so no link or token can ride along. The undo: `isUuid` on the id, `undoConsoleImport`, `{ok, cancelled, kept}`.
- **The shared view**, `app/(app)/team/import/import-view.tsx`, now takes `mode: "team" | "console"` (team by default; `ImportMode` in `import-lines.tsx`). The console's words come from `admin.peopleImport`, never the business's "team" wording group: the title and subtitle, the one-by-one line and problem ("ב„הוספת אדם”"), an expired invite renewed from the business's people ("חידוש ההזמנה"), the done card's `done.console` in place of `done.team` and `done.later`, its `removedKept` and `removeFailed` (the owners delete on /team; the team retries). In console mode the done card is followed by "ההזמנות מהרשימה": everyone the database made or updated, by its answer, never the preview's, with the number in one `ltr` piece and the role as a badge (accent above a worker): a `PersonList` on a phone and a tablet, a `DataTable` (שם | נייד | תפקיד) on a computer. No link is made there. A refusal `elsewhere` maps the rows' places back to the lines sent and their numbers, kept in a set the check reads (`againstTeam`'s new third argument), so each such line is to fix ("המספר כבר בצוות של עסק אחר"), whatever is edited around it, until it is removed; the alert says how many and that an accountant joins from "הוספת אדם", and the first one's "הסרת השורה" takes the focus, since the press is held. Because the console's actions read the page again (`refresh()`), a second list ("הזמנה מרשימה נוספת") is read against the invites the first one made; while an import taken back is held for its "ביטול", its invites are left out of the team the screen reads (by id, for as long as the page hasn't read it again), so its lines read as new at once, not as "כבר ברשימת ההזמנות". Done is built once (`doneOf`) for an import and for the twin's opening states.
- **@domain/people-import.** `PeopleProblem` gains `elsewhere`; `againstTeam(parsed, known, elsewhere = new Set())` turns a line it would write (new or an update) whose number is in the set into that problem, with its name, role and pair kept; a line left out is never one, as the database asks only what it writes.
- **The links.** `BusinessView` takes `importHref`: the checklist's staff item ("הזמנת הצוות") leads to the import, the owner's still to `people/new?role=owner`; beside the people's count a ghost link "הוספה מרשימה" (`admin.setup.people.import`, with `ListPlus`), so the page's one accent button stays "הוספת אדם". Both pages pass it, the console's under `staff.invite`.
- **Words** (he and en, same keys and placeholders): `admin.setup.people.import`; `admin.peopleImport.{title, subtitle, role.oneByOne, outcome.expired, problem.oneByOne, problem.elsewhere, done.console, done.people, done.toBusiness, done.removedKept, closed.title, errors.elsewhere, errors.removeFailed}`. "מוזמנים אחד אחד" as part 1's `role.oneByOne` has it, not the plan's "מזמינים".
- **The twin**, `/design/console/businesses/[slug]/people/import`: the Team twin's `ImportTwin` (now with `mode`, `paths` and `elsewhere`, the numbers its stand-in refuses as the console's database does, by place) on the console twin's business and its people (members, and its invites with or without a link), with the Team twin's pastes and states (empty, whatsapp, sheet, problems, again, tooMany, done, removed, `&fail=import|undo`), plus `elsewhere` (a shop's sheet with the owner of another of the console twin's businesses, in `data.ts`, listed in `tooling/hebrew-literals.ts` with its reason) and `closed` (the demo card). The stand-ins write nothing and nothing pasted leaves the device.

(2) The done card's list took each person's role from the preview (`roleOf`), so an invite made meanwhile in another tab, read as new and answered `updated`, showed the list's role while the database kept the invite's own: `writtenRoleOf(status, person, line)` in @domain/people-import gives the role by the database's answer (a new invite or a line naming its role: the role sent; an update from a line naming none: the invite the screen read, else null, and no badge), with 4 unit tests. The answer itself still carries no role, which would need a migration; an invite the screen read whose role changed meanwhile still shows the role read. (3) On a computer, a refusal for another business's staff with the table filtered to "יוזמנו" hid the marked line and left the focus nowhere: `LinesTable` is keyed by `elsewhere.size`, so each refusal draws it anew with its filter on "הכול" before the focus moves, and the refusal's own line is the last place the focus may go, never the body; a desktop case in design.spec. (4) The console's done screen was one column on a tablet too: with its list it now takes the editor's grid, the card at the start and the people at the end (the table at xl), the extra widths gone; design.spec checks the two side by side at 820 and 1440, one under the other at 390. (5) Too many invites waiting told the ConceptApp team to delete old invites on the Team screen, which it has no way to: `admin.peopleImport.{status,errors}.tooManyOpen` ask the owners to, in all three places, and the twin's `&fail=open` refuses that way for a design.spec case. (6, 7) A wrong list can be taken back only from the done card, and the console can't delete an invite: in console mode a line before "מחיקת ההזמנות מהרשימה" (`admin.peopleImport.done.removeHere`, the button's description) says so, and that the owners delete them one at a time on the Team screen afterwards. To ask Orel (6): a row "הרשימה האחרונה" on the console's business page, with the same held delete and "ביטול", from the import id in the last `invite.import` row (`admin_import_undo` has no time window and cancels only invites with no link, so no migration). `pnpm check` 5332 tests; Playwright not run here.
- After the check (five findings, all real): (1) `pnpm build`, Playwright, and so the screenshots at 390, 820 and 1440, are still not run here: this run's rules allow no build, dev server, Playwright or Docker. CI's build and e2e are the first run of `console-people-import.spec.ts` and of the new design.spec cases, and the console spec writes `console-import-1..4` at each size into CI's report. What `pnpm check` covers of it: it runs `next typegen` before `tsc`, so the two new routes' `PageProps<…>` are typed against Next's own route list. (2) Part 3 squash-merged as #271 (fce2feaa, on #270), so origin/main is merged in: docs/ROADMAP.md and packages/db/README.md conflicted only because both sides rewrote the same lines from f84bb68, and main's lines are part 3's head as this branch had them, so the branch's side holds both ("חלקים 1–4 מתוך 5"; the db README's paragraph with the console's screen as its first caller). The merged tree differs from main only by this PR's 26 files; the first bullet says part 3 merged. (3) The e2e bullet said the platform admin follows the checklist's link; the spec checks its href and follows the people's link, and the bullet now says so. (4) The console spec runs `expectCleanPage` (axe, no overflow) on two more states: the list taken back, with "ביטול" above the editor and the paste read as new, while it holds (8 seconds; axe moves neither the focus nor the mouse); and the same list again, `nothingNew` with its five "כבר ברשימת ההזמנות, בלי שינוי" shown. (5) The demo card's title said the business takes no invites from a list, while it takes none at all (`private.closed_to_invites`; "הוספת אדם" answers `demo` there too): `admin.peopleImport.closed.title` is "לעסק הזה לא מזמינים אנשים" ("This business takes no invites"), and the route bullet above quotes it. `pnpm check` 5335 tests.
- After the second review (nine findings, all real; the fourth only for its scroll): (1) An import taken back was left out of the team only while the page's `known` was the object read at the press ("for as long as the page hasn't read it again", above). Next 16 answers an action before it applies the page it read again (`server-action-reducer.js`), so a press in that moment tied the hide to the team from before the import, and the import's own reading, arriving after, dropped it: the five read as "כבר ברשימת ההזמנות" for the hold, and a list pressed again then would have cancelled them and counted them as skipped. The hide is now the import's invite ids alone, `withoutTakenBack` (`app/(app)/team/import/taken-back.ts`): they are left out while they still have no link, whichever reading the page has, until "ביטול", a failed undo, the next import or "הזמנה מרשימה נוספת" clears it; after the undo they are cancelled and gone from `consoleKnownTeam` anyway, and one that got a link meanwhile stays. 5 unit tests (the readings from before the import, after it and after the undo all read the same; a linked one kept; one the import didn't make never left out; nothing taken back, the team as read). (2) `admin.peopleImport.done.removeHere` said the delete together ends once you leave the screen, but "הזמנה מרשימה נוספת" ends it too, on the same screen: it now names both (he: "…רק כאן, לפני „הזמנה מרשימה נוספת” ולפני שיוצאים מהמסך הזה. אחר כך הבעלים…", and en). The console spec checks the words name that button, and that "מחיקת ההזמנות מהרשימה" is gone after it. (3) An `elsewhere` refusal changes the counts with nothing typed, so 600 ms after its alert the screen said them again: a layout effect takes those counts as said as the refusal arrives, so they are said again only after the next edit or removal. The console spec notes the counts the refusal leads to (`noteIfSaid`) and finds them unsaid a second later. (4) The refusal was brought into view and then the focus went to the marked line's "הסרת השורה", at the top of the preview, so on a phone the page moved twice and ended away from the refusal: now the focus moves first, and the refusal is brought into view only when it didn't go to a marked line, so the page moves once, to the line, its reason beside it. design.spec notes what `scrollIntoView` brings into view: never the alert for `elsewhere`, the alert for `tooManyOpen`, with the press focused. The rest of that finding stays: the alert and the focus in one commit, and a press with no reason of its own, are as every refusal had them, and the button's description is optional. (5) The real route's closed card was never reached (the twin draws the card directly): the console spec now first closes its business to invites as a hosted demo is (`closeConsoleImportToInvites`, a `private.demo_businesses` row with `'{}'`; demo-restaurant takes invites in the seed, so the suggested demo route would show the form), opens the import, and finds the title, the card's words, no field and the way back, then opens it again (`reopenConsoleImportToInvites`); `closeConsoleImportBusiness` also removes such a row. (6) The caret check for "תיקון השורה" held without the button doing anything, since `fill` left the caret at the last line's end: the spec puts it at the field's start first. (7) "Read again with the undo done" passed on the hidden set alone: the spec waits for the undo's own action response (a POST with `next-action` to the import's path, carrying the import's id) and finds the accountant's number in the team it carries (`refresh()`), and none of the five. (8) The refused line staying marked across edits was untested: the spec takes a line above it out of the field (it is line 8, still marked, the press held for four), puts it back, then removes it and brings it back with the toast's "ביטול", still marked. (9) The done list's numbers and roles: the manager's row has its number and "ניהול" and no "צוות", a worker's its number and "צוות", in the list on a phone and a tablet and in the table on a computer. `pnpm check` 5340 tests; Playwright not run here.

- Checked against the code at ae3a5705, where it differs from the plan or the task: (1) Part 1 added the outcome `locked` (an invite with no link the viewer may not change); the database has no such status (part 1's `PeopleImportStatus` has the plan's seven), so it answers `invited`, and the parity test reads `locked` as `invited`. (2) The plan's first pass held every row to the giver's rules and the console's elsewhere check, so a line for a member's number, or one holding an invite the import leaves (an accountant here on another staff, a stale screen's role), could refuse the whole import, against "left out whatever its role or name". Now the first pass checks only the rows' shape, and the rules and the elsewhere check apply to what is written, on the role it ends with: the line's for a new invite or one whose line named its role, the invite's own (checked as `touchable`) for the rest. (3) A pending invite whose named role was archived since: the screen reads it as not `touchable` (`canResendInvite` takes live roles), while the plan's SQL would have renamed it, and with a role on the line revived it. It is left as it is (`invited`), and `consoleKnownTeam` says so too; the plan's risks named the case. (4) No rows is 22023, not 54000 `limit`: an empty request is malformed, not too many (Zod refuses it before anyway). (5) The index has no `claimed_at is null` condition: the undo's `joined` reads the claimed rows too. (6) The log keeps `invited`, `expired` and `kept` apart, where the plan summed them into `invited`. (7) The undo has no demo check, as `team_invite_cancel` has none: a demo closed to invites can't have an import's invites, and one closed after an import can still take it back. (8) The undo's parameter is `batch` (the import's id), and the audit details' key stays `import`. (9) The team's doors also refuse an actor off the staff (an accountant) at the door; `gives_role` would refuse every write anyway. (10) The task said to hand-write only the functions' entries in the types; the generator also writes `import_id` in the invites' Row, Insert and Update, and CI's diff would fail without it, so it is there. (11) The plan's activity words read "בלי הזמנות חדשות, ועוד אחת עודכנה" for a list that only updated; the line says what it did (a nested plural): "הזמנה מרשימה: 3 הזמנות עודכנו". (12) The plan asked about a membership before locking the number's invite, so a claim of that invite committing in between would have left the import making a second, new invite for someone who had just joined; the invite is locked first now, the claim waited for, and the membership it made seen. (2) From the console, an invite with no link that the list only updates is held to the elsewhere check too (it is written), so a renamed line for a number now on another staff stops the list until it is removed. (3) An invite whose role the business archived since is left as it is by a list, never renamed or revived; "קישור חדש" or deleting it on /team is the way. (4) The undo has no time limit and no demo check, and any team.edit holder who gives every role among the import's waiting invites may run it (the plan's 8.8).
- After review (six findings, all confirmed, each fixed; what each changed is in the bullets above): (1) an imported invite to a named role stopped holding it after its 14 days, since `public.archive_business_role` counted an invite only before its date: the migration replaces it, and `readRoleInvites` counts the same; (2) no test held a line naming the role of an invite with no link to the giver's rules: two pgTAP refusals; (3) the package README said 54000 for no rows; (4) the hash check looked up the empty token's hash, and the notices check counted the joins without naming them; (5) the undo's `kept` counted the claimed too: it answers and logs `{cancelled, kept, joined}`; (6) ROADMAP's row said the plan, not what there is now. pgTAP from 59 to 64, `$$` from 12 and 76 to 14 and 82.
- After the check (four findings, all confirmed, each fixed): (1) the bullets above still said what the code did before review (the undo's `{import, cancelled, kept}` with the claimed among the kept, `$$` 12 and 76, no `archive_business_role`, pgTAP 59 and `{cancelled: 2, kept: 2}`): they say what it does now, and "After review" lists its findings only. (2) `importPeopleToTeam`'s doc said to pass `againstTeam`'s `send`, whose rows keep their `line`, which the strict `peopleImportRows` refuses (`changed`, nothing asked): the doc says to drop it, as the view does (`send.map(({ line: _line, ...person }) => person)`), and the unit test sends a row with its `line` and asks nothing; the parse stays strict, since it is the rows' boundary. (3) `PeopleImportRow.invite` said null only for a member's number: it says null also for an `invited` row whose new invite met a single invite made at the same moment (the import never reads that one's id; the parity test's case), and the unit test reads such an answer; the SQL is unchanged. (4) `consoleKnownTeam`'s stand-in ignored every filter: it records each as `column op`, and the test asserts the four reads' and the names' (the business, `active = true`, `claimed_at is null`, `archived_at is null`); with `archived_at` dropped from the code it fails. Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check` (5310 tests). Not run here: pgTAP, `pnpm test:db`, `pnpm build`, Playwright.
- After CI (94f5206): the migration and 63 of the 64 assertions passed. Test 45, "and the person joins by it", read the membership the claim had just written in the same statement as the claim: one statement reads the database as it stood when it began, so the membership wasn't there for it. The claim and the membership are now two assertions (65); the database doesn't change.
- After CI (32fec97): pgTAP passed whole (65). `supabase db lint`, which fails on a warning, found two in `private.import_invites`: its arrays `seen` and `far` began as the untyped literal `'{}'`, which plpgsql_check reads as text cast to `text[]` and `integer[]`. They are `'{}'::text[]` and `'{}'::integer[]` now; the migration's 14 `$$` are as they were.
- After the second review (seven findings, all confirmed, each fixed in the tests; the migration doesn't change, its 14 `$$` as they were; pgTAP still 65, its `$$` from 82 to 74): (1) the parity setup read the clock twice for a deleted invite's `claimed_at` and `cancelled_at`, which `invites_cancelled_closed` holds equal, so a millisecond's tick between them failed the insert: both are the transaction's `now()`. (2) Nothing held the lock order (the number's invite locked before the team is read): a fourth race holds a claim of the number's invite open while an import with that number waits on the invite's row, then answers `member`, no invite id and no import, the claimed invite the number's only one; with the two reads swapped it would make a second invite (`created`). (3) and (6) No pending invite past its date was counted or updated, and the update's 14 days were already the wizard's: 22 goes past its date before the owner's paste and comes out `updated`, never `expired`, its date 14 days on (asserted), and 99 of the cap's 499 waiting are past their date, so `54000 open @499` needs `link_pending or`. (4) The console's elsewhere check had no test for an update or for a row it leaves: B's worker's invite in A, renamed, refuses the list (`23505 elsewhere @2`), and once that invite has its link the same list goes through (`created invited`). (5) Six "nothing was written" checks after a `said_by` refusal could never fail, since its exception block takes back all the call did: dropped, the comment says why, `count_in` and `counted` gone with them. (7) Their places: a list of 300 taken whole (F's first 300 waiting), a manager's undo of their own list of workers, and the worker editor raised to hold every settable permission, where managers don't see pay (asserted), leaving the wizard's manager invite, renamed on the line, as it is (`invited`, the row unchanged), which only the rank clause decides. Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check` (5310 tests). Not run here: pgTAP, `pnpm test:db`, `pnpm build`, Playwright.
- Next: PR 4, the console's import (`/admin/businesses/[slug]/people/import`, with `consoleKnownTeam` and `importPeopleFromConsole`), then PR 5, `/team/import`.

The failing runs took 7.5 s (desktop) and 8.5 s (phone), about 3 s to line 361 and then the 5 s wait: no slow runner. The function broke its own word ("until it lands"), and nothing else puts the focus back: a read only reads the cookie, only a slide seals it again, and only activity slides.

- **The reader, `@domain/people-import`.** `parsePeopleList(input, roles, fallback)`: past 200,000 characters `tooLong` and nothing read; digits as 0–9 first (Arabic-Indic and Extended Arabic-Indic, `westernDigits`); the delimiter chosen once from most non-empty lines (a tab; else `;` or `,`, whichever more lines hold, a tie to the one written more often, then `;`, read by a hand-written quote-aware split where a quote counts only at a cell's start, `""` is a quote and a quote never closed reads that line as free text; else `|` or a spaced dash, never between digits; else free text); each raw line split before its cells are cleaned, empty cells kept. A header is a row with no mobile before any row with one, holding a header word exactly (`עובד`, `עובדת` and `employee` only beside another) or two cells that name columns; rows before it are `title` lines. Its cells are classified exactly first (the plan's lists, plus `שם עובד/ת`, `טל נייד`), then loosely (`מספר פלאפון`, `שם העובד המלא`, `טלפון הורה`, `מס' ת"ז`), so a second phone header blocks as the plan wants. Two phone columns, headed or by majority, give `phoneColumns` and no lines. With a header the phone comes only from its column (else by majority among the unnamed and unknown ones), the role only from its column (a word there that names no role in `dropped`, the list's role given: after review (10)), the name from the name, or first and last, columns; without one the columns are found by most of their cells, a second text column joins the name only when both hold single words (and, after review (12), it varies and the phone isn't between them), and every column left is named in `ignored` (`{header}` or `{column}`). A phone cell allows Excel's dropped zero; free text needs 0, 972, +972 or 00972. A run that is no mobile is split on its spaces and up to six groups from its start tried; every other run of 5 digits or more, and Excel's `5.01E+08`, go to `dropped`; a word naming the number beside it (`ת.ז`, `נייד:`, `טל'`, `ID`, never `טל` alone) leaves with it. Roles at the end, then the start, longest name first, in the plan's order (the business's names, `builtInRoleWord`, one by one, above rank); a blocked choice gives `givesPay` or `held`. Names through `parseTeamName` (`noName`, `nameTooLong`), `reversed` refused never turned; an initial keeps its dot, a geresh at the end stays. (3) The done card's `done.team` and `done.later` speak of "יצירת קישור" and "עוד לא קיבלו קישור", which PR 2 draws: until then the twin's done card names buttons that aren't there yet. (4) A line's removal keeps its "ביטול" for 8 seconds, as a deleted invite's. (5) "הסרת # השורות בלי מספר נייד" on a computer too, above the table, so every shape has the same actions. (6) The button reads "הזמנת אנשים" while nothing can go yet. (7) Roles on a line (the plan's section 8, question 4, which Orel kept as a default on 25.11 for the decisions page after its PR, and which this PR builds): one role for the whole list, and a line may name another of the business's staff roles, its named roles included, in a role column or at either end of a line; owners and accountants always one at a time. The alternative: one role for the whole list only, and a role written on a line changes nothing. (8) A sheet's "תפקיד" column holding job titles ("קופאית", "אחות", "מחסנאי"), after review (10): built as each such word left out on its own line ("לא נקלט: קופאית") with the list's role, a real role word in the column still read. The alternatives: the whole column not read when most of it names no role, said in `paste.ignored` (its real role words not read either); or every such line stopping until it is fixed, as before review. This changes the plan's technical default 14.
- After review (21 findings, all confirmed; 18 fixed in the reader, the screen or its words, and the three for Orel are defaults (7) and (8) above, for the decisions page): (1) A header "שם" beside "שם משפחה", "משפחה" or "Surname", with no first-name column, reads "שם" as the first name and joins the two, the first name first, when "שם" holds single words; before, every surname was left out, said only in `paste.ignored`. "שם מלא" beside "שם משפחה" is not joined. (2) "טל" makes a row a header only beside another header cell, as "עובד" does, so a first line "טל, 03-…" is a person's (`invalidPhone`), not the header that made every line under it `noPhone`. (3) A list typed by hand (`,`, `;`, `|` or a spaced dash, not a sheet's tabs): a line with no delimiter is read as free text, with any delimiter, and with no header a line whose phone cell holds no mobile while the line holds one by free text's rule (0, 972, +972, 00972) is read as free text too, so "מיכל אברהם 054-…" among comma lines, "052-…, יוסי לוי" and a dash list's number first all read; of two columns holding mobiles, the one filled on more than half the lines is the phone's, so "דנה כהן, ניהול, 050-…" above "name, phone" lines no longer gives `phoneColumns`. Lines read so take no part in finding the role and name columns or in `ignored`; a bare 5… is still no mobile. (4) A tab in at least half the lines (it was more than half) chooses tabs, and a sheet's single row is read by its cells, so "דנה כהן\t000000018\t050-…\t48" is "דנה כהן", columns 2 and 4 named in `ignored`, alone or under a typed line (which stays `noPhone`); before, the wage went into the name. (5) The header search reads a cell with letters by free text's rule, so a title's company or ID number ("ח.פ. 514789632") no longer ends it. (6) Name headers in other words: "השם", "שמות …", "עובדים", "איש צוות", "אנשי צוות", "worker(s)", "staff", "employees"; under a header with no name column, the one column of words headed in words nobody listed ("צוות") is the name, and with two such columns nothing is guessed (`noName`, as before). (7) An unsent invite the viewer may not change has its own outcome, `locked`: "למספר הזה כבר יש הזמנה שאי אפשר לשנות מכאן, והיא נשארת כמו שהיא" (en "…an invite you can't change from here…"), left out and counted with the rest (the summary's `leftOut`, the done card's `skipped`); `invited` is an invite with a link only. Not "רק הבעלים משנים": an invite above a worker's rank is a manager's to change too. (8) `nameKey` is `toLowerCase()`, as menu-import's, so a device in Turkish compares names as any other. (9) `NUMBER_LABEL` adds סלולרי, סלולארי, cell, and "מספר" or "מס'" before טלפון, נייד, פלאפון and סלולרי; `NUMBER_MARKER` takes a list's "1 " and "1 - " before letters ("054 111 2233 מיכל" still reads its number first). (10) A word in a role column that names no role of the business no longer stops its line: it goes to `dropped` ("לא נקלט: קופאית") and the line gets the list's role (`roleFromLine: false`); a role the viewer can't give from a list (one by one, above rank, pay, held) still stops it. The problem `unknownRole` and its words are gone. Default (8). (11) A headed phone column whose cells are mostly not mobiles, or empty, is not counted: the headed column of mobiles is the phone, and the other is named in `ignored` ("טלפון" of landlines beside "טלפון נייד"). Two headed columns of mobiles still give `phoneColumns`, now with `phoneHeaders`, and new `status.phoneColumnsHeaded` names them and says to delete, in the sheet, every column that isn't the person's own, and copy again (adding a header changes nothing there); `status.phoneColumns`, for no header, says "את העמודה שאינה של האדם עצמו" in place of "השנייה", and "איזו מהן". This narrows the plan's 2.1, which blocked any two headed phone columns. (12) With no header, a second column of single words joins the name only when at least half its cells are words of their own (a position or a department repeats, surnames mostly don't) and the phone's column isn't between them; else it is named in `ignored`, so "דנה | 050… | קופאית" is "דנה", no longer "דנה קופאית". (13) "תיקון השורה" puts the caret at the line's end with nothing selected: on a phone the first key typed replaced the whole line. (14, 19) `summary.header` names the header's line, "שורה <ltr>{line}</ltr> נקראה כשורת הכותרות." ("Line … was read as the column headers."), from its `kind: "header"` line: the sheet's twin said "the first line" of line 3. (15) `role.oneByOne` and `problem.oneByOne` say "מוזמנים אחד אחד", as the English twin and ROADMAP do. (16–18) Defaults (7) and (8) above. (20) With nothing new to invite, the summary says new `summary.update` ("# הזמנות יתעדכנו", "# invites will be updated"), as the button says "עדכון # הזמנות"; the counts said aloud follow. (21) Any edit of the field takes the removal's toast away, so its "ביטול" never brings back the text from before what was typed since; "תיקון השורה" leaves it, as it changes no text. Tests: unit `people-import.test.ts` (99, from 81: שם with שם משפחה, משפחה, Surname and before it, a full name beside a surname, a family's two surnames kept, positions past the phone and repeating beside the name left out, name headers in other words and under one nobody listed, two such columns guessing nothing, a sheet's one row alone and under a typed line, Excel's dropped zero in it, a title with a company number above the header, "טל" first in three shapes and beside a header, headed landlines and an empty headed column, `phoneHeaders` with headers and without, three hand-typed lists and one with a role on a line, a bare 5… there, four more labels, list numbers with a space or a spaced dash, a name line and a labelled number line paired, job titles in a role column left out and an owner there still stopped, names compared under Turkish's lower case, `locked` beside `invited` and `expired`); each new test failed against the reader as it was (21 failed). The twin's states read as before (checked: 7 to invite from WhatsApp, 6 from the sheet with its header on line 3, 8 to fix, 2 to update the second time). e2e `design.spec.ts`: the caret at line 5's end with nothing selected; a keystroke after a removal takes its "ביטול" away; the sheet's summary names line 3; the second paste's summary says 2 invites will be updated and never "2 אנשים יוזמנו"; two headed phone columns named in the status. Docs: the package README (a sheet's single row, lines typed by hand, `phoneHeaders`, a role column's unknown word, "שם" with "שם משפחה", the second column's join). Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check` (5215 tests). Not run here: `pnpm build`, Playwright (CI is the first run of the changed e2e).
- After the check (7 findings; 6 fixed, 1 left for before merging): (1) Pressing "הזמנת # אנשים" while an import taken back is still held for its "ביטול" sends that undo first and waits for it (`held.commit()` in `importNow`). Before, the new import went first, found the invites the first one made still waiting and updated them ("לא נוספו הזמנות חדשות"), and then the late undo cancelled them all. When that undo fails, its done card comes back and the new import doesn't go. The twin's stand-ins now keep each import's invites waiting until its undo cancels them, as the database would, each import under its own id, so the twin shows this. (2) A column with no header is named by its place in its own sentence, new `paste.ignoredAt` ("עמודה 3 לא נקלטת.", "עמודות 1 ו-4 לא נקלטות.", en "Column 3 is not read."), beside `paste.ignored` for the columns a header named. `paste.column` is gone: it gave "העמודה עמודה 3 לא נקלטת." The sentences come from `ignoredWords` (new, `app/(app)/team/import/ignored-words.ts`). (3) A problem line keeps the name it read before its problem (a role it can't be given, `duplicate`): `name` on the problem line. A member's line says "כבר בצוות, בשם …" only when the line's own name is known and differs, so the owner's own "בעלים" line says "כבר בצוות". (4) "תיקון השורה", "הסרת השורה" and "הסרת # השורות בלי מספר נייד" do nothing while a keystroke has not been read yet, just as the import's press waits: their line numbers come from the rows, which were read from the text before it. (5) The same number twice is the same person (`sameLine`) when the name and the role are the same, whether the line wrote the role or took the list's: `roleFromLine` no longer makes it a conflict. The first line, which is the one sent, counts as naming its role when either line does, so an invite waiting takes the role a line wrote. (6) The sheet's e2e now checks the whole `paste.ignored` sentence for ת.ז. and שכר לשעה, not just any text with "ת.ז.", which the computer's table also drew in the header line. (7) Not done: `pnpm build`, the e2e, and screenshots at 390, 820 and 1440 (this run may not start a build, a server or a browser). CI's e2e runs against `pnpm build`; the screenshots are still needed before merging. Tests: unit `ignored-words.test.ts` (new, 3: a column with no header in Hebrew and English, two named by place, headers and places together), `people-import.test.ts` (+1: the list's role written on one of two lines, in both orders, and the role an invite waiting then takes; a duplicate's and a refused role's name kept). e2e `design.spec.ts`: taking an import back and inviting again at once makes the 7 again, with its own way back; the owner's own line says "כבר בצוות", never "בשם אורי בן דוד"; the sheet's exact `paste.ignored`. Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check` (5219 tests). Not run here: `pnpm build`, Playwright.
- Run here before the PR, against a local `pnpm build` of 6089de2 (the twin needs no database), at 390, 820 and 1440: every import test in `design.spec.ts` (54), axe in light and dark included. One failed on phone and tablet, on its own words: "the twin reads a sheet by its header" found the fold by "הצגת השורות" again after pressing it, when it says "הסתרת השורות"; it now finds it by either and checks each name with its `aria-expanded`. The screenshots showed the desktop table's phone column breaking a number at its dashes ("054-", "710-", "4401"): the phone is kept in one piece (`whitespace-nowrap`) in the table and in the list rows. The app doesn't change otherwise.
- After the second review (23 findings, all confirmed, several of them the same from two lenses; 22 fixed, 1 left): (1) A foreign number is never read as an Israeli mobile from its end: a run of digits that starts with another country's code ("+380 50 …", "+38 (050) 123-45-67", "+995 555 …", "+33 5 …", "00380 50 …") is one foreign number, in `dropped`, its line `invalidPhone`; and Excel's dropped zero is read only from a cell's first group, so "380 50 123 4567" in a phone column is no mobile either. Before, the national part was read as +9725…, in a sheet's column and in free text, and nothing on screen said so. (2, 17) A name's header word exactly ("שם העובד", "Employee name", "Name") comes before one in other words wherever each sits, and a first name's column before a name's header in other words; "שם …" or "… name" for a branch, a department, a manager, a parent, a user, a company, a shop or a role ("שם הסניף", "שם מנהל", "שם המחלקה", "שם האב", "Username", "Branch name") is never the person's, and "שם תפקיד" is the role's column. Before, the first of them won and everyone was invited under the branch's or the manager's name. (3) A role on a line is a field of its own, parted from the name by a separator, a colon or the number ("דנה 050-… בר", "בר: דנה כהן"), or the last words of a name that keeps two words ("דנה כהן ניהול 050-…"); at a line's start only with a separator after it. So a named role called like a first name or a surname ("בר", "ברמן", "טבח") is no longer read off "בר כהן", "דנה ברמן" or "יוסי טבח, 050-…", which gave that role (one built on manager too) and half a name. A line that is a role's name and a number is still `noName`. (4, 23, and 7's WhatsApp paste) WhatsApp's time, date and sender before a message ("[10:15, 26.11.2026] אורי בן דוד: ", "26.11.2026, 10:15 - אורי בן דוד: ", iOS's "[26/11/2026, 10:15:32] …" and the US "11/26/26, 10:16 PM - …") are left out of every line that carries them, before the cells are chosen (a message's next lines carry none), and the sender's own number is never the person's. (5) The refusal's focus runs only as a refusal arrives (`arrived`, as the invite form's), never again when the screen is shown from its <Activity>. (6, 12) The counts are said only when they changed since they were last said: not after "מחיקת ההזמנות מהרשימה" or on an <Activity> re-show; "הזמנה מרשימה נוספת" starts afresh. (7, 20) A number in free text stays in the name only as one or two digits right after the name's words, before the mobile ("דנה כהן 2", as pinned); any other goes to `dropped` ("לא נקלט: 45"): a wage after the mobile ("45", "38.5", "8,500", "₪7.200"), a year ("1990"), an age in brackets ("(17)"), an ID's check digit ("12345678/2"), and a number in a cell of its own on a line typed by hand ("יוסי לוי, 24, 052-…", a single comma or pipe line too: free text now joins a line's cells with a tab). `NUMBER_LABEL` adds "מס' עובד", "שכר", "שכר לשעה", "wage" and "salary", and a passport's label leaves with its letters ("דרכון PA1234567"); a currency sign or a slash left at a name's edge goes. With no header, a second column of single words joins the name only when most of its cells hold no digits ("45ש״ח" never does). A single typed line is still free text (the finding's other way), its wage gone all the same. (8) A line's removal toast, and its "ביטול", go as an import is made, taken back, or "הזמנה מרשימה נוספת" starts: pressed behind the done card it brought the old list back and left the focus on the page. (9) "ביטול" after a removal gives the focus back to what removed the lines once their rows are drawn again, in view: the line's "הסרת השורה" (`data-remove-line`) or "הסרת # השורות בלי מספר נייד" (`data-remove-no-phone`); its time up with the focus on it, the line now in its place, else the preview's title, or the done card's. Never the field, off screen on a phone, its keyboard opening over the lines. (10) A dash of any kind beside a digit (U+2010–U+2015 and U+2212: WhatsApp's U+2011 inside direction marks, Word's en dash) reads as "-" in `rowsOf`; a name's own dash stays, and `pasted-text`'s `clean`, so the menu's reader, is unchanged. (11, 19) In a tab paste, a row of one filled cell that holds a mobile (a title's "איש קשר: 050-…") no longer ends the header search: the header under it is found, and the title is a title, not a person to invite. (13) "N הזמנות כבר קיבלו קישור, ולכן נשארו" is said politely as it appears. (14) The refusal is drawn after the lines, beside the status line above the press, and brought into view as it arrives. (15) Tablet and computer: the stuck column of the role and the paste is capped to the screen and scrolls on its own (as the chat's and the orders' columns), so the field "תיקון השורה" focuses comes into view. (16) Not done: each removal's words said twice (the toast's live region, then the focused "ביטול"'s description) is `@ui/status-toast`'s, which this PR doesn't change, and every screen with `focusAction` has it (12); its fix (the region quiet and the words in the button's name, as `UndoRow`) is a change of its own to the component, its tests and /design. (18) A typed list's second column of mobiles, filled on some lines and beside the first one's on a line, is a phone column all the same: `phoneColumns`, as with tabs, so a parent's number is never read as the person's on a line whose own cell is empty. (21) `peopleImportRows` refuses a row with anything more (`text`, `dropped`, `line`). (22) The twin takes `&fail=import` and `&fail=undo`, whose stand-ins refuse. Defaults, for the decisions page: (9) a number kept in a free-text name is one or two digits right after it, before the mobile; (10) a run of digits that starts with another country's code is one number, so a line with "+44 20 … 050-…" and no separator between them is `invalidPhone` until fixed; (11) a tab paste's line of one cell with a mobile above a header row is a title. Tests: unit `people-import.test.ts` (109, from 100: foreign numbers in a phone column, without a header and in free text, the dropped zero from a first group only, 00 972 and +972 still read; five dashes and U+2011 in marks and contacts' pairs, a name's en dash kept; a title with a contact's mobile above a header, a row of cells with one still ending the search; six pairs of name headers before and after the person's, a father's beside first and last names, "שם תפקיד" as the role's column; a column of sums never joined; a sparse second column of mobiles; eleven numbers out of free-text names, single comma and pipe lines, a typed line read as free text; four WhatsApp prefixes and a sender's number; names equal to named roles and those roles read when parted; `peopleImportRows` with three extra keys). Each new reader test failed against the reader as it was (9 of 9); the rows' extra keys pass on both, a guard. The twin's pastes read as before (checked: the same lines for whatsapp, sheet and problems). e2e `design.spec.ts`: "ביטול" after the headings and after line 5 gives the focus back to their buttons, line 5's in view; the WhatsApp test records every request and none carries a number of the list; new, the counts said once and not again after taking back, and a removal's "ביטול" gone with the import; new, `&fail=import` (the refusal after the lines and in view, the press focused, a new alert for the same refusal) and `&fail=undo` (inviting again while "ביטול" is up brings the done card back with `errors.removeFailed`, the focus on "מחיקת ההזמנות", and no second import). Not tested: (5) and the <Activity> half of (6), (13) (the twin keeps no invite) and (15). Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check` (5228 tests). Not run here: `pnpm build`, Playwright (CI is the first run of the changed and new e2e).
- After the third check: 10 findings, all confirmed, three of them the same from two lenses, so 7 fixes, all made. (1, 7) `NUMBER_LABEL` takes "עובד" only after "מס'" or "מספר" ("מס' עובד 12"), and "שכר" only with a colon or "לשעה". So a bare "עובד" before the number is the line's role again: "דנה כהן עובד 050-…" is a worker from the line, never the managers a list was set to. And "דוד שכר 050-…" keeps his surname. A wage written "שכר 45" after the mobile now leaves "שכר" in the name; the number still goes. (2, 10) A run is no longer foreign when "+" comes before a 0 ("+054-…", "+0527654321", in free text and in a phone column), since no country's code starts with 0. Nor when "00" and at most one digit (1, 7) come before a group with 0 first ("001 050-…", "מס' עובד 001 050-…", "דנה כהן, 001, 050-…"): the mobile is read and "001" goes to `dropped`. "0038 050 123 45 67", "00 38 050 …" and "00380 (0)50 …" stay foreign, now pinned, since Ukraine's 050 is written as Israel's. So the finding's wider rule (any group with 0 first) was not taken; see default (12). (3, 8) Only a named role of one word that no built-in role is called (`nameLike`: "בר", "טבח", "מארחת", "Host") keeps the two-words rule, and is read at a line's start only as a field of its own. A built-in role's word or the business's word for one ("ניהול", "מנהלת", "בעלים", "עובדת", "צוות") and a role of two words ("אחראי משמרת") are read at either end again, after a one-word name too: "דנה ניהול 050-…", "ניהול דנה כהן 050-…", "דנה בעלים 050-…" (one by one), "דנה עובדת 050-…" (a worker on a managers' list). (4, 9) First and last names come before a name's header in other words only when both columns are there ("Nickname"). Beside a first name alone, the full name's column is read ("שם העובד המלא", "Employee full name") and the first name's column is left out. "שם המשפחה" and "השם הפרטי" are the last and first names' headers. (5) A typed list's second column of mobiles blocks (`phoneColumns`) only when it sits beside the first on a line and also holds the number on a line with no mobile of its own in the first. When every line's own mobile is in place, it is left out and named in `ignored`, as before the second review. (6) "הסרת השורה" and "הסרת # השורות בלי מספר נייד" take `ignoreHeldEnter`: Enter held on "ביטול" brings the lines back and doesn't remove them again from the button the focus returned to. (9) "תפקיד" and "role" are out of `NOT_THE_PERSON`. A role's name ("שם תפקיד", "שם התפקיד", "Role name") is read as the role's column by `ROLES_NAME`, tested on the header with its marks. So a name and a role together ("שם / תפקיד", "Name / Role", "Name (Role)", "שם + תפקיד") is the name's column again, and its lines are not all `noName`. Default, for the decisions page: (12) "00" and a code of two digits or more before a group with 0 first ("0038 050 …") is a foreign number. So an employee's number of four or five digits with zeros first, right before the mobile with no separator ("0012 050-…"), is `invalidPhone` until fixed. Tests: unit `people-import.test.ts` (112, from 109). New: an employee's number with zeros first and a stray plus; a full name beside a first name alone, in both orders, with "Nickname" beside first and last, "שם המשפחה" and "השם הפרטי"; seven roles read off a one-word name or at a line's start, two owners, and four worker words on a managers' list. Added to tests already there: three more foreign numbers; "Role name" and four headers of a name and a role; a typed line with two mobiles above four; "דוד שכר"; "שכר:" and "שכר לשעה"; "ברמן דנה כהן" and "דנה מנהלת" beside named roles. Seven of the nine tests new or changed failed against the reader at 43a4e600; the foreign numbers' and the wage's pass on both, as guards. e2e `design.spec.ts`: Enter held on line 5's "ביטול" takes the line back, and the line stays with its "הסרת השורה" focused. Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check` (5231 tests). Not run here: `pnpm build`, Playwright (CI is the first run of the new e2e step).
- Run here again after the second review and the third check, against a local `pnpm build`: the import's 60 tests in `design.spec.ts` at 390, 820 and 1440. Before this commit one failed at every width, on a real bug of the second review's: the counts were said only when they changed after the first edit, and the first edit was known by a ref the effect never ran for, so typing that left the counts as they were (a space after the twin's list) said nothing at all. The edits are now a count in state the effect reads: each pause after typing says the counts unless the same were said already, and starting a new list counts from 0 again. All 60 pass.
