## 2026-11-26 · chat · inviting from a list (הזמנה מרשימה), part 2: who hasn't got a link yet, on /team

- On chat/pending-links from origin/main 0578e09e (#265). The plan's PR 2 of section 7 ("מסך הצוות: מי שעוד לא קיבל קישור", its 1.6, 3.1 and 3.6), under Orel's decisions of 25.11.2026: each person's link is made at the moment of sending ("יצירת קישור", then WhatsApp or a copy), /team keeps "עוד לא קיבלו קישור", and the app sends nothing to anyone. Built beside PR 1 (chat/import-reader: the reader, the words of the paste and the import twin), which isn't merged; nothing here needs it. One migration, no new npm package, no new `packages/ui` component (`SendLinkRow` is made of existing ones and lives in app/, as the plan says), so no /design gallery entry. **Left for PR 5, as asked: the import twin's send-list state** (`/design/shell/team/import?state=send`), which draws PR 1's `PeopleImportView`; the /team twin's pending rows are here.
- **Migration A**, `20261128000200_invite_links_pending.sql` (no branch on origin or here uses the number; main's newest was 20261128000100): `invites.link_pending boolean not null default false`, with its comment. `private.mark_links_pending()` (sql, security definer, `search_path ''`, revoked from public, anon and authenticated) marks once every unclaimed invite that no `invite.create` or `invite.send` row of its own business names (`details ->> 'invite'`), and the migration runs it; on a local database the seed comes after, so nothing is marked there. The trigger `invites_link_given` (`before update of token_hash`, `private.invite_link_given()`, plpgsql, `search_path ''`, revoked the same) clears the flag when an open row gets a new hash, so `team_invite_link`, `team_invite`'s refresh, `issue_invite_link` and `admin_invite`'s renewal clear it and none of the four is redefined; `team_invite_cancel` closes the row in the same update and leaves it; a write of the same hash leaves it; nothing sets it back. `public.create_business` is 20261020000100's copied whole (`create or replace`, the same signature, so its grants stay service_role only), with its invites saved `link_pending` true and a comment. New, `public.team_invite_first_link(actor, invite, new_token_hash)` (plpgsql, security definer, `search_path ''`, revoked from public, anon and authenticated, granted to service_role only): it locks the open invite `for update`; while `link_pending` holds it returns `public.team_invite_link(...)`, unchanged, with every check it makes; once not, it refuses 42501 to anyone who doesn't edit that invite's team (`private.holds_perm(…, 'team.edit')`, which asks for an active membership) and P0002 with hint `linked` to anyone who does; an invite no longer open is left to `team_invite_link` (P0002, no hint); of two presses at once the second waits for the lock and is refused. `team_invite_link` isn't redefined, and "קישור חדש" still calls it, as it replaces a link on purpose. `public.admin_business` (20261117000700's copied whole; checked with diff) gives each person `link_pending` (false for a member) and an invite with it the status `invited` whatever its date, listed with the open ones: its statuses stay the three the code before reads. `public.admin_businesses` (20261107000900's; only its two counts differ) counts such an invite among the open ones, never the expired. Both `create or replace` with the same signatures, their grants given again. `$$` counted with `grep -o '\$\$' | wc -l`: 12 in the migration (six bodies), 44 in the pgTAP, each even. RLS unchanged: the invites' one select policy and the table grant cover the column. `packages/db/database.types.ts` by hand, as the generator writes it: `link_pending: boolean` in Row, `link_pending?: boolean` in Insert and Update, between `invited_by` and `named_role`, and the function `team_invite_first_link` between `team_invite_cancel` and `team_invite_link`; no existing function's signature changed.
- **@domain.** `TeamInvite.linkPending?`; `InviteState` gains `pending` (`inviteState`: pending whatever its date, so the wizard's invites never read "פג תוקף"); `buildTeam` puts them first, by name, unnamed last, then id (`byNameThenId`), and `counts.pending`, with `open` and `expired` counting only invites with a link (the subtitle's "# הזמנות ממתינות" and the expired hint say what they said). `TeamProgress.pending`: `teamProgress` counts every invite waiting for a link, whatever its role (a second owner's and an accountant's too), as `counts.pending` does, while `joined`, `invited` and `expired` stay on the staff, so when the step is done is unchanged. `NO_TEAM_PROGRESS`; `teamStepWords` (pure): while any wait for a link it says so, after who joined once one of the staff did (`donePending`, plan 3.1), else alone (`pending`); with none waiting, `done` or `doneWaiting` once one joined, else `waiting`, `expired`, `none`. `firstStepHref` leads to /team while any wait. `whatsappToHref(phone, text)` in share.ts: `https://wa.me/<digits of the E.164>?text=…`, throwing on anything not E.164; the text names nobody.
- **@db.** `openInvites` reads `link_pending` first, then (missing column) the named-role read, then the oldest, each `readAllPages`; `getTeam` gives `linkPending` (false before the migration). `issueFirstInviteLink` in @db/invites calls `team_invite_first_link` and reads `linked` before `teamInviteError`. `getBusinessSetup` in @db/admin parses the three statuses with its Zod enum, as before, and `link_pending` (default false, so a database before the migration reads as before), and gives `SetupPerson.status` `pending` for an invited one with it.
- **The screen.** `app/(app)/team/send-links.tsx` (client): `PendingLinks` (inside `InviteLinks`, so a link made in one shape is the one every shape shows) holds a row from its press until the answer (`hold`), so it stays in place, by name, after `firstLinkAction`'s `refresh()` reads the invite as open, until the page is loaded again; an answer that made no link and isn't "linked" lets it go (`release`, `holdsAfter`), and a held row stays only while the screen still reads its invite, in any state (`pendingShown(rows, held, invites)`, `here` the held ids still read). `TeamPage` keys `TeamView` by `${userId}:${businessId}`, lower-cased, as `ChatProvider` is keyed, so nothing held, made or waiting for its undo outlives the person or the business (cacheComponents keeps React state across both). `PendingList` (phone and tablet, its own PageSection above "הזמנות", with `PendingCount`: "# אנשים עוד לא קיבלו קישור", or "לכולם כבר נוצר קישור" once all are made, and the hint while a link is left to make), `PendingTableRows` (the desktop's first rows, with an accent "עוד אין קישור" badge, or "ממתין להצטרפות" once made, and the link column), `SendLinkRow` (through `firstLinkAction` → `issueFirstInviteLink` → `team_invite_first_link`, which `TeamView` takes as `firstLink`, by default its `resend`, so the twins and the test need nothing; a `useActionState` form as `ResendLink`: "יצירת קישור"; once made, "וואטסאפ" as a real `<a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">` through `Button asChild`, which takes the focus, else the announcer says "הקישור של {name} מוכן"; then `CopyLink`; after WhatsApp or a copy the row says "נפתח בוואטסאפ" or its copy button "הקישור הועתק", and the focus goes to the next person's "יצירת קישור" in the same shape, coming round to the top, with "הבא בתור: {name}" said). Refused as "linked" (a link was made meanwhile elsewhere, and still works): the row says `app.team.link.errors.linked` in place of its press, in every shape (`PendingLinks.elsewhere`), counts as having its link (`useLinked`: the count, the desktop's "ממתין להצטרפות" badge, skipped as the next), and the line takes the focus the busy press lost, else the announcer says `app.team.pending.linked`; it stays in place until the page is loaded again. `UnlessPendingHere` / `UnlessAllPendingHere` / `WhileAnyListed` / `ListedUndo`, so the open invites leave out a row held above. Pure parts in `pending-links.ts` (`pendingShown`, `holdsAfter`, `released`, `nextToLink`, `leftToLink`, the `data-pending-create` and `data-pending-links` selectors). A row the viewer can't link (a role an owner gives, `ownerOnly`, or one the viewer doesn't give) says "את הקישור להזמנה הזאת שולחים הבעלים."; one whose named role was removed says the existing roleGone line; a reader of the team gets the rows alone; every row keeps "מחיקת ההזמנה" by the rank rule. `TeamView` takes `businessName` (the message's) and `whatsappToNumber` (false by default: only the real page passes it). `invite-cancel.tsx`: `InviteCancelUndo` with `ids` draws only its own invite's "ביטול" and refusal (the refusal keeps its invite), since a shape has two lists now, and takes `strays` (every invite the screen read): `Invites` draws one above both lists, which says only a refusal of an invite neither has ("gone" after the refresh), never a "ביטול" (`cancelSaid(id, what, where)`, pure, `invite-focus.ts`); `useGoneInvites`. `CopyLink` takes `onCopied`. `resend-link.tsx` exports `useInviteLinks`. Today's team row says "# אנשים עוד לא קיבלו קישור" first, then the invites waiting, then the team's size. The console's people (`business-view.tsx`) draw an accent "עוד אין קישור" and "הבעלים שולחים את הקישור ממסך הצוות." above its own "יצירת קישור הזמנה", which stays, since its hint says to send the link it makes (the trigger then clears the flag); the checklist's count keeps them, as it keeps every status but expired.

- Checked against the code at 0578e09e, where it differs from the plan or the task: (1) `firstLinkAction`, as `resendInviteAction`, calls `refresh()`, so a row keyed on the `InviteLinks` map alone would leave the list the moment the screen is read again, maybe before the link's own state lands; the row is held from the press until an answer makes no link. (2) `InviteCancelUndo` drew its "ביטול" in every list of a shape whatever `ids` it had; with two lists a deleted pending invite would have had two, both taking the focus. Now each list says only its own. (3) The plan's ListGroup for phone and tablet: `ListRow` has no place for actions under its text, so the rows are the team's own `PersonList`/`PersonRow`, as the invites under them. (4) The plan's `createFor` ("יצירת הקישור של {name}") and `whatsappFor` ("שליחת הקישור של {name} בוואטסאפ") didn't hold the words the buttons show, which a voice user says (WCAG 2.5.3); they start with them now, as "קישור חדש ל{name}" and "מחיקת ההזמנה של {name}" do: "יצירת קישור ל{name}", "וואטסאפ ל{name}, עם הקישור" (en "Make the link for {name}", "WhatsApp {name}, with the link"). (5) The task said demo businesses take no invites; on the local seed both do (seed.sql:152, :182), and only the hosted ones are closed. The spec uses businesses of its own anyway, as first-steps.spec does, so no demo is touched. (6) The plan's e2e drives the wizard's screens and the console's InviteLink; the spec calls the same functions as the seeded platform admin instead (new-business.spec and invite.spec drive those screens), which saves a sign-in and its code per size; the owner and the staff member join through the screens. (7) Today's team row said "# הזמנות ממתינות" for the wizard's invites through `counts.open`; with `open` now only invites with a link it would have fallen to the team's size, so it says those waiting for a link first (not in the plan). (8) The plan's facts held: `create_business` is defined only in 20261019000100 and 20261020000100 (20261115001500 defines `create_business_role`), `team_invite_link` never asks `expires_at`, every link writer logs its invite, `openInvites` was a two-step `isMissingColumn` read, `teamProgress` counted `open` as invited. (9) The plan's 3.1 line ("the console checklist's staff item counts members only and needs nothing") is wrong, as the checklist counts every invite that hasn't expired: to correct where the plan lives, outside this repo.

- After the second review: five findings, each confirmed in the code and fixed; the bullets above say the final state. (1) `firstLinkAction`, and `resendInviteAction` on main before it, sent the invite's id to Postgres unparsed, though the row binds it in the browser: any string reached `team_invite_first_link` and came back as 22P02, which `teamInviteError` doesn't know, so the thrown message, the posted text in it, went to the server log. No data or rights were reached (the database checks team.edit on the invite's own business). `issueLink` now refuses anything `isUuid` doesn't take as `notOpen` before the session or the database is asked (`isUuid` rather than `z.uuid()`, which refuses some ids Postgres takes, such as `…-4000-c000-…`), and logs each write by its own name (`issueFirstInviteLink failed`, `issueTeamInviteLink failed`); `app/(app)/team/actions.test.ts` (new). (2) Nothing tested the app's side of "linked": `issueFirstInviteLink` reading P0002 with hint `linked` before `teamInviteError` (which says `notOpen` for any P0002), and `firstLinkAction`'s refresh on it. `packages/db/invites.test.ts` now stands in for the database (`issueFirstInviteLink`), and the action test covers the refresh. (3) pgTAP never asked `team_invite_first_link` for an invite no longer open, which the app reads as `notOpen` by its missing hint, and named a test for more than it proved: now a first link for the deleted invite and for `gen_random_uuid()` is refused P0002 with no hint (a guard that let a closed invite reach the team.edit check would answer 42501), and the worker's refusal says it shows the delegation to `team_invite_link` (plan 45 to 47; `$$` still 44, and 12 in the migration, which is unchanged). (4) `getTeam`'s fallback for a database before `link_pending`, which the hosted demo reads between its deploy and Migrate, had no test: `packages/db/team.test.ts` (new), against a stand-in that answers 42703 for a column the database lacks. (5) Today's team row's "# אנשים עוד לא קיבלו קישור" had no test: `team-pending-links.spec.ts` checks the row says 3 before any link and 2 after the join. Mutations checked: dropping the `linked` check in `issueFirstInviteLink`, the `isUuid` refusal, the refresh on `linked`, and making `openInvites` throw on 42703 or still name `link_pending` in its fallback each fail a new test. Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check` (5160 tests). Not run here: pgTAP, Playwright (the e2e step's first run is CI's), `pnpm build`.

- After review: seven findings, two of them the same, each confirmed in the code and fixed; what was built is in the bullets above. (1) A held row outlived its invite: after Dana's link was made here and she joined, the next link's `refresh()` read the team without her invite, yet her row stayed with its dead link, its WhatsApp and a "מחיקת ההזמנה" answered "gone"; and nothing keyed the screen, which cacheComponents keeps across a switch of business or a new sign-in, where a held row of business A drew A's person, number and live link on B's screen, and its delete, bound to B, passed `changer(B)` and cancelled A's invite (`team_invite_cancel` checks the actor against the invite's own business). Hence `pendingShown`'s `invites` and the key on `TeamView`. (2) A stale "יצירת קישור" replaced a link already sent (two findings, the correctness and the security lens): `team_invite_link` never reads `link_pending`, and nothing reads /team again on its own, so of two people with the screen open the second's press made a new token and the link the first had sent on WhatsApp stopped working. Hence `team_invite_first_link`, refused as "linked", and the row that says so. (3) The "gone" refusal was lost on phone and tablet: each list says only its own invites' since part 2, and after the refresh the invite is in neither, while the desktop's table, which says every one, is hidden there. Hence the line above both lists (`strays`, `cancelSaid`). (4) This entry's `$$` count for the pgTAP said 30; it was 34 then. (5) The console said "הוזמן", and after 14 days "ההזמנה פגה" with "חידוש ההזמנה", for invites nobody got a link for, while /team said "עוד אין קישור"; and its staff item turned "עוד אין צוות" then. Hence `admin_business`'s and `admin_businesses`' changes and the console's badge and line. ## 2026-11-25 · chat · the card and the tab say who is away during the shift (known issue 107), and the remove button's hours in order

- On chat/away-card from origin/main 5fa9cfd (#263), and since the check merged with origin/main 3ad643db (#264, part 5 of the fill, as it was squash-merged), so it lands on main after #264. Two small display fixes in one PR: no migration, no SQL, no new package, no new `packages/ui` component (so no /design gallery entry), no new message key. Its edits sit next to #264's in `schedule-builder.tsx` and `week.ts`: this PR changes `schedule-builder.tsx` in its import line and the card's `away` prop alone, and `week.ts` in `currentPublishedSchedule` and `PublishedSchedule.away`'s doc alone, none of them near #264's lines (`fillWeekHours` in `ScheduleBuildWeek`, `currentScheduleBuild` and the builder's props); the fill's code is untouched.
- **Known issue 107, the card and the tab.** A shift's card in the builder (`ShiftCard` in `build/shift-card.tsx`, its `away` prop from `schedule-builder.tsx`, which read `awayOn` on the shift's day inline) and the schedule tab's two cards (`ShiftCard` in `shifts/shift-card.tsx` on phones and tablets, `WeekShift` in `week-board.tsx` on a desktop, both through `awayOnShift` in `shifts/shift-text.tsx`) now read who on the shift is away during it as its sheet and the copy do: `assigneesAway(spans, shift)`, new in `@domain/absences`, the shift's people for whom `awayDuring` holds, in the shift's order. So someone already on a night past midnight into a day away is said so on its card, in the builder and on the tab, where nothing was said before; a shift that ends at 00:00 exactly stays its own day's only.
- **The words.** Both cards say `app.build.awayOnShift`, which is now "{names} לא בעבודה בשעות המשמרת" ("{names}: away during the shift"), the sheet's words (`groups.away`, `notes.away`), in place of "{names} לא בעבודה ביום הזה" ("{names} away that day"), for every day away, a day shift's too. The value changed in place, in both files: no key of the sheet's takes names. The builder's card names first names (`shortNames`), the tab's full names, as before; the day's own line ("לא בעבודה: {names}", `awayByDay`) still reads the day alone, so a night into a day away is said on its card and not on its day.
- **The tab's read.** `currentPublishedSchedule` read days away over this week and next only (`addDays(thisStart, 13)`), so a night on next week's last day missed a day away on the day after. It reads `awayWindow(thisStart, 2)` now: `awayWindow` takes a number of weeks (one by default, the builder's, unchanged), those weeks and the day after the last, fifteen days within `team_away`'s 62. Who reads days away and never why is unchanged: `teamAwayFor` gives them only to whoever builds the schedule, the tab passes null to everyone else, the cards draw the line for managers only, and `AwaySpan` has no kind.
- **The availability's remove button.** The "×" of a range in the day's sheet is an `IconButton`, whose label is both its name and its native tooltip. It said "הסרת השעות {from}–{to}" as plain text, so the tooltip, drawn in the page's direction, showed "09:00–17:00" as "17:00–09:00". `app.availability.removeRange` now holds the hours in `<ltr>` (he and en), read with `t.markup` and the sheet's own `isolate` (LRI U+2066 … PDI U+2069), as its UndoRow's message already was. That `isolate` moves out of `availability-sheets.tsx` into `availability/range-words.ts` (new, no React), with `rangeWords(range)`, the values of both sentences that name a range (`removeRange`, `sheet.removed`). Screen readers read the name in its own order before and after (the isolates are format characters, not read); only the tooltip was drawn backwards.
- The sweep for others: every range in he.json is in `<ltr>` but three. `app.clock.absences.rowLabel` ("{kind}, {from}–{to}") is a `DataTable` row's `aria-label` only, never drawn, so it stays; `app.lists.schedules.dayRun` joins Hebrew day letters, which read right; `shell.*`'s "18–24", "6–7" and "1–5" are the old /design shell's words on screen, no name or tooltip, left as they are. The six `t.markup(…, { ltr: (chunks) => chunks })` calls each name one time, not a range, and `IconButton` is the only native tooltip fed hours.
- Tests: unit `absences.test.ts` (`assigneesAway`: a day shift on its own day, a night into the day after beside its own day's, a shift until 00:00 and an evening not, the last day away counting for its own night and not the next, the shift's order, nobody on it, no days away; `awayWindow` for two weeks: the day after next week read, which the old window missed, a year's end inside, one week as the default), `shift-text.test.ts` (`awayOnShift`: a night into a day away, a day shift and one until 00:00 on the shift's day only, the shift's order with someone no longer on the team as such, nothing without days away; the sentence is the sheet's words in he and en), `range-words.test.ts` (new: the button's label in he and en with the range in one isolate and no digit outside it, a night's range, the removal's line, `isolate` itself). The tests of a night into the day after, of the tab's window and of the label fail when their change is put back (checked by mutating `assigneesAway` to the shift's day alone, the two weeks' window to one day short and `isolate` to nothing: 4, 1 and 4 tests failed). e2e `absences.spec.ts`: the night into a day away the day after the week, copied and Noa put on it, now says so on its card too; a new test saves next week as a draft with Noa on Sunday's morning (away that Sunday) and on Saturday's night 20:00–02:00 (away the Sunday after next week), and both cards say "נועה לא בעבודה בשעות המשמרת" in the builder (on a phone, Saturday's own line names nobody away) and "נועה כהן לא בעבודה בשעות המשמרת" on the tab's next week (the day picked below a desktop's width), with axe and a screenshot of each at each width; it waits for the builder's heading before any click, and puts the schedule back (days away are cleared before and after every test of the spec). `builder-focus.spec.ts`: the twin's "×" of 09:00–17:00 is found by its new name, which holds the range in isolates, and its `title` is that label.

- After the second review (two findings, both confirmed, both low): (1) Typed digits in a settings number field saved only on a blur that reached React, and nothing else saved them: since the first review the field has no timer, so a value typed and not left (a screen reader's cursor moved on, then the way back, a closed tab, or another app in front without a blur) stayed unsaved, while the screen kept in its <Activity> still showed it. `useDraft` now also saves a whole draft that isn't what's stored when the page is hidden (`visibilitychange`) or closed (`pagehide`), and when the screen is left (a layout effect's cleanup, which runs as Next hides the screen in its <Activity> and on unmount), as `useHeldWrite` does; still no timer while typing. Leaving again after a refusal, with nothing typed since, still tries again, as before; typing clears what was sent. It covers every `useDraft`: the settings form's number, text and time fields, the business's name, address and colour, and quiet hours. The flush goes through the same server actions, which set no cookie of their own, so a write POSTed after the screen is left still lands (the forwarded-action note); a closing tab's is best effort, as `useHeldWrite`'s is. (2) Enter on a number outside its ends blurred the field, and leaving went back to what's stored without a word. Enter now keeps the draft and the focus and says the ends ("מספר בין 4 ל-58", through the page's polite announcer). Leaving the field with a draft outside them still goes back to what's stored, and says so: new `app.manage.numberNotSaved`, "{name}: לא נשמר, כי צריך מספר בין {min} ל-{max}. נשאר {value}.", with its English twin. Enter on a number within its ends blurs and saves, as before; `TextField`'s Enter is unchanged (every draft is whole there, so nothing is dropped), and where the focus lands after Enter is as it was. Tests: unit `use-draft.test.ts` (new, `sendsNow`: a draft nothing sent goes either way; left, then the screen goes: nothing again; gone with the screen, then left: nothing again; left again after a refusal: tries again; another draft, or another stored value: goes). e2e `manage.spec.ts`: 60 and Enter keeps 60, the focus and `aria-invalid`, and says the ends; Tab goes back to 42 and says `numberNotSaved`, with nothing saved. New: 50 typed, the focus kept, saves as the page is hidden (`setPageHidden`); 52 saves as the screen is left by the way back; two activity rows in all, and the way forward shows 52. Default applied, to ask Orel: the words of `numberNotSaved`, and that Enter on a number outside its ends keeps it rather than going back. Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check` (5058 tests). Not run here: Playwright (CI is the first run of the changed and new e2e), `pnpm build`.
- After the third check: three findings, all confirmed and fixed; the first and the third are one bug. (1, 3) A business's name or address that went as the page hid or the screen was left, and was refused (no signal, the page frozen mid-fetch), could not be sent again by leaving the field: `sendsNow` held back every later blur and hide against a draft that went `away`, and only a keystroke cleared it. The name and address keep what's stored until a save lands, so the typed text and its "השינוי לא נשמר" stayed on screen while leaving the field, as the form's doc and the second review's bullet promise, did nothing. Now `save` may say how it went: `BusinessDetailsForm`'s `change` returns whether it saved (a promise of a boolean, as `SettingsForm`'s already does), the name's and the address's drafts hand that on (no `void`), and `useDraft`'s `send` gives it to `forgetRefused` (pure, beside the hook), which forgets the draft sent once its save says false or fails, when it's still the last one sent. So the next blur or hide sends it again, however it went; one still on its way, or saved, still holds the blur back, as before. A save that says nothing (quiet hours) is as it was; the settings form's fields follow their optimistic change back after a refusal, so nothing was stuck there. (2) Enter on 60 keeps it, outside its ends, beside its minus; a press of minus blurred the number first, which put back 42 and said "נשאר 42", and the press then stepped from 42, so 41 saved (43 with plus). `Stepper` takes `keepsTyping` (a new prop, off by default, so the other steppers are as they were; no new component): a press going down on minus or plus while its number has the focus leaves the focus there (`keepTypedFocus` prevents the mousedown's focus change, which a finger's tap goes through too), so no blur comes first, and the press steps from what's typed, into its ends: 60, then minus, 58, saved soon after as any press is. The settings' `NumberField` sets it. Chosen over passing the blur's `relatedTarget` through, since Safari and iOS give the press's button no focus, so the blur there names nothing. Tests: unit `use-draft.test.ts` (`forgetRefused`: a refused or failed away-send forgotten, so leaving the field and the next hide send it again; one sent as the field was left, too; kept once saved, for a save that says nothing, and while it's out; another draft sent since kept), `stepper.test.ts` (the focus kept in its own number and left alone elsewhere; both buttons hold it with `keepsTyping`, neither without; 60 between 4 and 58 steps to 58 with minus and nothing with plus). e2e `manage.spec.ts`, new: 60, Enter, then minus: 58, the focus still in the field, saved once, and no `numberNotSaved`; an address typed, its save as the page hides aborted (`page.route`), the failure on screen and the address kept, then Enter once the page is back saves it, and the failure goes. Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check` (5084 tests). Not run here: Playwright (CI is the first run of the new e2e), `pnpm build`.

## 2026-11-25 · chat · filling the schedule from availability, part 6: sample availability in the hosted demo

- On chat/demo-availability from origin/main 39ce451 (#260). The plan's PR 6 (§3.6), after Orel's yes to its question 3 (25.11.2026): the hosted demo restaurant's manager and two workers have next week's availability sent, so whoever shows "מילוי מהזמינות" there has someone to fill from, and it comes back after every reset. Independent of part 4 (#262, the pill and the sheet, still open) and touches no screen: one migration, no new package, no new words. On the screens it shows as availability already sent: Shiran, Noa and Yoav find next week's form filled in, and "זמינות הצוות" shows all three as sent.
- Migration `20261128000100_demo_sample_availability.sql` (the number is free on every branch and open PR on origin). `public.renew_demo_availability()`, security definer, `search_path ''`, revoked from public, anon and authenticated, granted to service_role: for each `private.demo_businesses` row whose definition has a `sampleAvailability` array, with scheduling on, next week by the business's own settings and clock (`private.availability_rules` and `private.week_start_after`, the reminders' own, which `reminders.db.test.ts` holds equal to `nextWeekStart`). For each person it names who is active on the staff as a manager or a worker (as `private.availability_missing` counts who owes availability: never an owner, an accountant or someone who left) and sent nothing for that week, it opens the week (`public.open_schedule_week`, due by `private.availability_due_at`), then sends the ranges through `public.submit_availability` with no note, so every rule of a sent answer holds; a published week gets nothing (checked under the week's `for share` lock, as a write next door takes it). Returns how many answers it wrote, 0 the second time. `private.demo_sample_ranges(business, week_start, given)` maps each weekday to its date in the week and cuts it for a business closed on Shabbat as the form's suggestion cuts it (`suggestedDay`): Friday ending by candle lighting, Saturday starting from havdalah, from the database's own table (`private.shabbat_span`, `private.closes_on_shabbat`); a range left with nothing, or on a Friday or Saturday past the table's last Shabbat (2031), is left out, and a person with nothing left gets no answer. Locks: the business `for key share` (a demo reset under way finishes first), the week, then every answer in one statement, and only then the week's reminders lock (after the second review, below).
- Never an answer someone sent: every named person's row is claimed first, in one statement (`insert … select … on conflict (week_id, user_id) do nothing returning user_id`), and only the rows claimed are filled by `submit_availability`; a check followed by `submit_availability` alone would replace an answer sent at the same moment, since that function upserts. An empty answer ("not available") counts as sent.
- Nothing else: `submit_availability` writes only the answer and its ranges; the only triggers on availability are `availability_staff_only` (refuses an accountant) and `availability_submissions_resolve`, which settles reminders (`private.settle_availability_week`: resolves or recounts existing lines, never inserts one), and a demo business never has reminders (`private.run_reminders` leaves demo businesses out); `open_schedule_week` inserts the week alone, and `schedule_weeks` has no trigger. So no row of the log, no notification, no push, and no SMS: nothing in availability sends one.
- Data, `supabase/hosted-demo.sql` only, on the restaurant's definition, with the ids it already uses (the owner, Uri, never): Shiran (manager) Sunday to Thursday 09:00–23:30 and Friday 08:00–14:30; Noa Sunday, Monday, Wednesday and Thursday 09:00–23:30 and Saturday 19:30–00:00; Yoav Sunday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Thursday 09:00–23:30, Friday 08:00–14:30 and Saturday 19:30–00:00. Each is the day's hours as part 3b's form suggests them from the restaurant's shifts (09:00–16:00 and 16:00–23:30, Friday 08:00–14:30, מוצ״ש 19:30–00:00), on the days each can work, so each covers every shift of those days. In a December week the fill places 16 of the 44 places with all three: Shiran 5 shifts (35.5 hours, Thursday stopped by the 42 hours), Noa 5 (34.5), Yoav 6 (39.5), one a day each; in June 14, since havdalah (20:32 in Tel Aviv) is after מוצ״ש's 19:30, so the Saturday night stays open with its Shabbat reason and Noa and Yoav's Saturday answers start at havdalah. `supabase/seed.sql` and `reset_demo_business` are unchanged, so the local e2e (Noa's single range) is too.

Not next to `renewDemoSchedules` inside `if (offered.renews)`: the restaurant doesn't renew its week, so the call runs on every entry, whichever business, and the database does nothing for a definition without the key. `packages/db/database.types.ts` has the function's entry in the generator's shape (as `renew_demo_schedules`'); `pnpm db:types` couldn't run here, and CI's database job compares.

- After the second review (five findings, each confirmed; the migration isn't applied anywhere yet, so it changed in place): (1) A lock cycle with the form. The first claim's insert fired `availability_submissions_resolve`, whose `private.lock_availability_week` takes the week's reminders lock (an advisory lock held to commit), so the renewal held it through every later claim. A form sending for a later named person who hadn't sent yet took that person's row and waited in its trigger for the lock, while the renewal's claim waited on the form's row; Postgres broke it after a second, usually in the form's trigger (which gives up settling and keeps the answer), else the whole renewal, logged by its caller. Now every named person with a range left is claimed in one statement (`insert … select … from jsonb_object_keys(named) order by id … on conflict do nothing returning user_id`), and `submit_availability` runs for the claimed ids only, so the trigger takes the lock only after every wait on another session's row. The migration's header and the package README name the advisory lock in the lock order. Otherwise as before: the week opened only when someone has a range left, nothing on a published week. (2) The door's tests had no `renewDemoAvailability` in their `@db/demo` mock, so each entry threw inside the new try/catch and logged it (7 times), and deleting the call passed every test. The mock has it now (3 by default, and in `nothingCalled()`), with four new tests: one call per entry into either business, after the shop's week, none when sign-in fails, and the person still lands when it fails, with its message alone logged. Removing the call fails three of them (checked by mutating `actions.ts`). (3) pgTAP's "no notification" and "no push" couldn't fail: X and O now have notifications on, so anything that would notify writes a line, and "no push" is gone, with a comment that it follows from no line (a push is sent only for one). (4) "An answer sent meanwhile is kept" was untested. pgTAP now has the worker replace the sample with hours of their own and a note between the calls, and the second call keeps them. The new `packages/db/demo-availability.db.test.ts` runs the renewal and the form's `submit_availability` in sessions of their own (its business committed, since both must see it, and taken away after): an answer being sent as the sample runs stays the sender's (1 written); one sent while the sample is being written replaces it (2 written); and with the week's reminders lock held by a third session, the sample's trigger waits, the form's answer waits on the sample's row, and once the lock goes both finish with no warning from the trigger, where claims one at a time deadlock. `tooling/running-week-edge.test.ts` checks the one claim before the one `submit_availability`, so it fails under `pnpm check` too. (5) The checks past the Shabbat table hard-coded 2032 dates, which stop being past it once the table is extended; they now take the Sunday after the last Friday the table has for X (`pg_temp.past_table`), and O's Monday after it. Left: the review's other half of (5), next week passing the table's end from 21 December 2031, since `tooling/shabbat-table.test.ts` has the table extended a year before that; and comparing the notifications by a snapshot rather than a count, since the fixture has no reminder line to update. pgTAP stays at 32 (one assertion out, one in). Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`. Not run here: pgTAP and `pnpm test:db` (no Docker here; CI's database job runs both, the new database test's first run), `pnpm build`, Playwright.
- After CI (979853f): pgTAP and every unit test passed; `packages/db/demo-availability.db.test.ts` stopped before its tests, in making its business: the definition went as `${JSON.stringify(DEFINITION)}::jsonb`, which postgres.js sends as a JSON string, so the row held a string, not an object, and `demo_businesses_definition_check` refused it. It now goes as `tx.json(DEFINITION)`, as `employee-files.db.test.ts` sends its patch; the three races run first on this CI.

## 2026-11-25 · chat · filling the schedule from availability, part 4: מילוי מהזמינות

- On chat/fill-button from 84464467: part 3's own commits (chat/fill-reads) merged with origin/main d754fbb (part 2, #258), not #260 as it was merged. Since the check the branch is merged with origin/main 39ce4516 (#253, #259, and #260's squash, which carries #259's rework of the "kept as suggested" test in `fill-week.test.ts`). The plan's PR 4: the pill, the sheet and the fill's way back in `/schedule/build`. No migration, no SQL, no new package, no new `packages/ui` component (so no /design gallery entry); in `@domain` one constant, `FILL_REST_HOURS` (8, `LIMITS.restBetween` in hours, the number the sheet names).
- **The pill.** On a draft with an open place in a shift still to start (`!published && !weekOver && gaps.people > 0`, the count behind "# מקומות פנויים"), "מילוי מהזמינות" (lucide `CalendarCheck`, aria-hidden) comes after "העתקה מהשבוע שעבר" in the status row. WeekBuilder now closes it as the screen is left, as the clock's panel ends a scan (`useLayoutEffect(() => () => setFillOpen(false), [])`): this covers the link, the browser's back and a tab. Drawn again, the closed sheet may slide away once, as a sheet closing when the screen was left does. (2) Pre-existing: after "יציאה בלי לשמור" the work let go (a fill, a copy, changes by hand) stayed on the kept screen with no unsaved mark, and on a draft "פרסום הסידור" would send it. `leaveWithoutSaving` now puts the screen back to the week as read (`letGo`: the rows from `read`, each with a new screen id; nothing removed or deleted meanwhile; no copy; no locked-back or clash line; nothing unsaved), and a copy's or a fill's "ביטול" goes with it (`onEdited`), since it would bring back what was there before it. `read`, `basis` and the marks stay, so shifts put back after a refused write stay as they are now. (3) The clock taking a focused control away, on a draft of the week running now as the last shift with an open place begins. The pill is `FillPill`, whose `useFocusWhenGone` hands the focus to where a fill's "ביטול" would go (the copy's pill), else the line that says where the week stands, never the page's body. The sheet's "מילוי # מקומות" is `FillButton`, which hands it to the footer's line (`tabIndex={-1}`), which then says nothing is left to fill, so the reason is heard and not only the sheet's name. (4) The leave sheet's focus as it closes. Asked from the fill's link, its recorded opener went with the fill's sheet, so a refused "שמירת הטיוטה ויציאה" (`weekSaved`, `weekMoved`, a clash) was read with the focus on the page's body. The leave sheet now has `returnFocus`: what had the focus as it asked (`leaveFrom`, recorded as the sheet records its own), else what opened the last sheet (the fill's pill), else the status line (`firstOnScreen`). A tab or the week switch gets it back as before. The finding's first part, "חזרה לסידור" landing on the fill sheet's ✕ rather than its link, is Radix opening the sheet afresh (the first control that isn't a link), not a defect, and is left. - After review (seven findings, each confirmed, in six points; two pairs were the same finding seen twice). (1) The clock and the sheet (findings 1 and 7). The sheet was drawn on `fillOpen && offerFill`, and on a draft of the week running now the clock can begin the last shift with an open place while it is open: `offerFill` turned false, the sheet slid away by itself with the pill, `fillOpen` stayed true, the focus fell to the page's body, and the next change that opened a place again (a "×" in a shift's sheet, a new shift) opened the fill's sheet unasked, over the shift's. Now the sheet is drawn, and worked out, on a draft alone (`fillable`, `!published && !weekOver`), and `offerFill` is `fillable` with an open place: so `fillOpen` can't outlive what is drawn. With nothing left open the sheet stays and says "לא נשארו מקומות פנויים במשמרות שעוד לא התחילו." (`fill.nothing.full`), with only its ✕; closed, the focus goes to its pill, else where a fill's "ביטול" goes (`afterScreen("fill")`: the copy's pill), else the line that says where the week stands (`closeFill`). Of the review's two fixes this is the second: it says why instead of going without a word, and needs no state set in the render. (2) The leave sheet over the fill's (findings 2 and 8). The reminder's link is the first link in a builder sheet, and with work unsaved the leave guard stops its click at the document and asks, with the fill's sheet still open under the question. The fill's sheet is now drawn only while the guard asks nothing (`guard.target === null`): it goes while the question is asked and comes back on "חזרה לסידור"; "שמירת הטיוטה ויציאה" and "יציאה בלי לשמור" clear `fillOpen`, so it doesn't come back on the way out. The other fix offered, closing it from the link's own click and asking through `guard.leave`, would need a capture handler, since the guard stops the click before the link hears it, and "חזרה לסידור" would then land on nothing. (3) Age (finding 4): `app.build.fill.age`, "המילוי לא בודק גיל. כדאי לבדוק לפני הפרסום מי מתחת לגיל 18." ("The fill doesn't check age. Before publishing, check who is under 18."), at the end of the rules paragraph in every business, clock on or off: part 3's recommendation. No rule and no limit, so decision 3 stands; default (2) above is now this line, with its question, and the new words of (4) and (1) are defaults (6) and (7). (4) The week running now (finding 5). Its answers were all sent before it began and nobody can send one now, so "אין עדיין את מי לשבץ…" promised what can't come, and with the reminder's link off the sheet named no next step. `fillLine` (`fill-reasons.ts`, pure) picks the footer's line: with nobody's answers and no reminder, "אף אחד לא שלח זמינות לשבוע הזה. אפשר לשבץ ידנית מתוך כל משמרת." (`nothing.noAnswersRunning`); and the line of who sent nothing ends with "אפשר לשבץ אותם ידנית מתוך כל משמרת." (`fill.byHand`) wherever the reminder isn't. Next week's words are as they were. (5) Enter held down (finding 6). Pressed from the keyboard, the toast's "ביטול" undoes the fill and gives the focus to the pill, which the undo brings back, and the key repeating there opened the sheet again. Both pills now take `onKeyDown={ignoreHeldEnter}`, the copy's too (the same gap on main), as the station's open button does after its own "ביטול". (6) Tests (finding 3, and each point above). Unit `fill-reasons.test.ts` (12, from 8): `fillLine` gives the note while anyone is placed; nothing left (`nothing.full`) once the clock begins the only open shift, at 09:00 and not at 08:59, whoever answered; nobody answered, waiting only with the reminder; and each shift saying why once anyone answered. e2e, not run here: `builder-focus.spec.ts`, the restaurant's pill now reached by Tab from the line that says where the week stands, not `focus()`; "ביטול" still focused after `SCREEN_UNDO_MS` and a second, as the first steps' spec waits out `HOLD_MS`; and Enter held down on it (a second keydown with no keyup) undoes once and opens nothing on the pill. New there, the shop's next week on the running twin: Tab from that line to the copy's pill, then the fill's (the plan's risk 13), and the twin's clock moved twelve days on with the sheet open: it stays, says `nothing.full`, has only its ✕, and Escape puts the focus on the copy's pill with the fill's gone and nothing opening again. `design.spec.ts`: the age line on the restaurant twin, which has no clock; new, a change by hand after a fill (Uri onto Sunday's morning) takes the fill's toast within two seconds (the review's optional point, through `applyFill`, `edited` and `onMade`), and the reminder's link on unsaved work shows the leave sheet with the fill's gone, back on "חזרה לסידור", and "יציאה בלי לשמור" goes to the team's availability with no sheet open. `schedule-build.spec.ts`, new: a draft of the week running now that nobody answered for (`saveDemoRunningWeek(NOA, 2)`: `needed`, new in `e2e/db.ts`, leaves a place open in today's evening): `nothing.noAnswersRunning` and not `noAnswers`, `byHand` beside who sent nothing, no link at all (the reminder's choice of week, untested until now), the age line, axe, and Escape back on the pill. Docs: the ROADMAP's fill row (by hand on the week running now, the sheet staying as the clock takes the last place, the age line in every business), the package README (the age line). ## 2026-11-25 · chat · the no-code sheet keeps its close until it has said why

- On chat/clock-sheet-flake from origin/main 9f8c04c (#256). `clock-code.spec.ts` "a press without a code refused as already in keeps its sheet until it has said why" (#251) failed now and then on the phone project (#256's first run, #253's run 45605317707): no alert in the dialog, as the sheet had closed. The cause is in the app, not the test. The sheet's close had two holds in turn: the press's (`useSheetWrite`'s `held()`, live, from the press until the answer) and the sheet's own (`closeHeldForRefusal`, read from what React last drew, from the refusal drawn until it is said). The first let go as the answer came (the `finally` after the write, a microtask), and the refusal was drawn only on React's next turn (a task of its own, `setNoneFailure` after an `await` being no transition), so a close in between found neither holding: ✕, the scrim, a swipe or Escape there closed the sheet with the refusal never said. A person can do it, an input coming in before React's turn on a busy phone. Reproduced on the bundled ClockPanel (React Compiler on, a Next-like action answering first and drawing the page read again 150 ms later, headless Chromium at 390, 820 and 1440 px, reduced motion): one Escape in a task posted in the answer's own task closed it 9 times in 9; the test's Escape every 5 ms 0 in 60 with the page idle as the answer came, 24 in 24 with it busy for 8 ms then (a frame, the stream), which is what the phone project hits now and then. #256 has no part in it. "חזרה לסריקה" had a gap of its own: enabled as the page read again is drawn, a drawing before the alert (`said` is set in an effect), and pressed then it closed the sheet with the refusal unsaid (9 in 9).
- `@ui/use-sheet-write`: a write gets `keep`, which it calls as its answer comes when the sheet says that answer only later; `held()` then lasts until the sheet calls `said()`. Kept before the write lets go, the hold passes on with no moment free. A new write lets go of what an earlier one kept (it holds the sheet itself). Without `keep`, as before (the hours' sheets keep too, in a second commit: Also, below).
- `ClockPanel`: the refusal without a code calls `keep()` before `setNoneFailure`, and passes `noneSaid` to the sheet. `WithoutCodeSheet`: `onSaid`, called in a layout effect once the alert is committed (before any other task can come in), and "חזרה לסריקה" disabled while a refusal waits to be said (`refusalUnsaid`, new in `clock/clock-focus.ts`; `closeHeldForRefusal` is built on it). Its own close guard stays. Sheet's `onOpenChange` doc names `keep` and `said`.
- e2e: the test's Escape every 5 ms is an Escape in every task (a MessageChannel loop, each posting the next), so one is always queued ahead of the task React draws in and comes between the answer and the refusal drawn: on the bundle at HEAD it closed the sheet 24 in 24 with the page idle (and 12 in 12 busy), where the 5 ms timer missed it. With the fix, every probe keeps the sheet until the alert in every run at all three sizes (the single Escape in the gap 33 runs, the 5 ms timer with the page busy 42, the loop 54, "חזרה לסריקה" pressed as early as it can be 9), and an Escape after the alert closes the sheet at once.
- Tests: unit `use-sheet-write` (held at every turn from a kept answer on, through the page drawn, until `said`; `said` doesn't let go of a write on its way; a new write takes over from a kept answer; the hook gives `said`), `clock-focus` (`refusalUnsaid`). The new `sheetWrites` tests fail on the hook as it was. Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`. Not run here: Playwright against the app, `pnpm build`.
- Also: the hours' sheets, the only other caller of `useSheetWrite` (`clock/team/person-view.tsx`), had that gap, one turn wide as the no-code sheet's: every write from them hands its refusal to its form after the `await`, a state React draws on its next turn, while `held()` let go at the answer. Reproduced on the bundled `PersonHoursView` (the same harness: React Compiler on, a Next-like action answering 60 ms after the press and drawing the page read again 150 ms after that, headless Chromium at 390, 820 and 1440 px, reduced motion), every write refused: one Escape in a task posted in the answer's own task closed the sheet with the refusal unsaid 60 in 60 (6 on each of the ten ways to send one), the MessageChannel loop 30 in 30. Fixed on every sheet, the same way: a shift added or fixed (`EntryForm`), cancelled (`CancelForm`), brought back from its own sheet (`CancelledForm`), a break changed, added or removed (`BreakForm`), and days away recorded, changed or removed (`AbsenceForm`, `absences-section.tsx`). Each write goes through `sendFromSheet` (new, `clock/team/sheet-answer.ts`), which calls `keep()` as a refusal comes (a write that failed to go too, said as "failed") and before its form is handed it; each form calls `said` through `useSaid` (new in `@ui/use-sheet-write`), a layout effect of each drawing that shows its refusal, so the ✕ pressed in the turn the refusal is drawn still closes the sheet at once (`clock-team.spec.ts`'s `closeOnRefusal`, 23 November; with a passive effect it was held, 18 in 18). Each drawing, not only the first: a refusal kept again in the same words draws nothing new. With the fix the sheet stays until the alert on every write at all three sizes (the single Escape 60 runs, the loop 60), and an Escape after the alert closes it at once, before the page read again. Left as they are: `ClockPanel`'s sheet (above); the toast's "ביטול" (`restoreShift` from the toast, `restoreBreak`, `restoreAbsence`), as it writes from no sheet (`startUndo`, not `useSheetWrite`), and a refusal there opens the sheet already saying it or says it in the toast; and the buttons inside the sheets that leave a form without closing the sheet ("ביטול המשמרת" and the break rows on the fix, "חזרה לתיקון השעות" on the cancel's and a break's steps), as they wait on `saving`, which lasts until the page read again is drawn, after the refusal: pressed in the first task they could be, the refusal came first every time (30 in 30 at HEAD, 30 with the page read again coming at once, 30 with the fix). Tests: unit `sheet-answer` (held at every turn from a refusal, or a write that failed to go, until `said`; the month handed on; nothing kept once done), whose first two fail without `keep()`; e2e `clock-team.spec.ts`: `closeOnRefusal` counts only an alert drawn in the sheet still open, and the shift added over another and the cancelled one that can't come back press Escape in every task from the press on (`escapeUntilRefused`) until the refusal: on the bundle that probe failed at HEAD 39 in 39 (9 with the page busy 8 ms as the answer came) and passes with the fix 78 in 78 (18 busy). Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`. Not run here: Playwright against the app, `pnpm build`.
- Checked, a second look at both commits and every caller of `useSheetWrite`: one sheet left that a kept refusal holds for good. A page left by back or forward while a write from one of the hours sheets is on its way is kept hidden in an Activity (Next's, with `cacheComponents`), which lets go of its refs; drawn again hidden (the write's transition ending, the page read again), `@ui/sheet` found its anchor gone and moved what it holds to the page's foot, drawing it anew: what was typed gone, and the refusal with the form it was handed to. Come back to, the sheet was open, empty, and held: ✕, Escape, the scrim and a swipe closed nothing until another write (for a record of days away, whose save waits for a change, only its removal). On the bundled page in an Activity (the business's colour outside it, as `(app)/layout.tsx` has it), hidden right after the press or in the gap after the answer, shown again once the page read again came, on a shift added, a cancel and days away recorded: at bebbca9 the refusal was lost 32 in 32 (the sheet still closed), at the hours' commit lost and the sheet held 32 in 32. Fixed in `@ui/sheet`: the place found from the anchor is kept in a layout effect and used while the anchor is let go, so nothing changes while a page shows (its first drawing as before, a sheet mounted open still moves on its first change); React still hides the sheet with its page (a portal under a hidden Activity is hidden too). With it the refusal shows in the open sheet with what was typed, and one Escape closes it, 32 in 32. Rerun with it: the hours probes (one Escape in the gap on all ten writes, the loop, the ✕ on the refusal, both together, the steps pressed as early as they can be) and the no-code sheet's (the gap, the loop, "חזרה לסריקה", the 5 ms timer with the page busy 8 ms) pass as before at all three sizes. The rest holds: no drawing can say a refusal before it is drawn (React draws a click's, a key's and the answer's updates together, and each form clears its refusal as it sends, the removal of days away setting a new one each time), no form can be switched or drawn anew in the sheet while it is held (its keys change only as a sheet opens, the steps wait on `saving`), a second write or a success lets go of what was kept, and a refusal that comes while the page is hidden is said as it shows again (a layout effect runs again then). `useSaid`'s doc says the form handed the refusal must stay drawn until it has said it. Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`.
- After the second review (six findings, each confirmed in the code; (1) to (3) older than this change, (4) to (6) on its tests): (1) A sheet left open by back and come back to by forward closed onto the page's body. As its page was hidden, Radix's focus scope let go and asked where the focus goes, and `@ui/sheet` forgot what opened it; shown again, React ran its layout effect again, which noted the body (or the page hidden by then) in its place. `@ui/sheet` now notes what held the focus only as it goes from closed to open (`wasOpen`), and, asked while its content is still drawn (only hidden with its page, not closed), moves no focus and keeps what it noted. A sheet that really closes, or goes with its page, is no longer drawn when it is asked, so it gives the focus back as before. (2) A removal of days away refused in the same words as the refusal showing (no signal twice, a month closed) changed nothing on the page, so its alert was never heard again: the removal now clears the refusal as it sends, as the save does. (3) A kind or days left out was read twice, as the focused field's description and as an alert. `AbsenceForm` now draws the refusal before it moves the focus (`flushSync`, so a refusal from the server is read with its field too), the days' line is no alert, and the kind's line is drawn under the kinds by the form itself, describing each kind rather than the list. `ChoiceList` is unchanged: a group's description isn't read with the focus on a phone's screen reader, so its alert would have given way to nothing there. (4) to (6) Tests: unit `sheet.test.ts` (a sheet still drawn moves no focus and chooses none; it fails on the sheet as it was); e2e `clock-team.spec.ts`, a sheet left by back and come back to by forward gives the focus back to "הוספת משמרת"; `absences.spec.ts`, a refusal on its way as her page is left by back is said once it is come back to, with what was typed, and the ✕ closes it onto "רישום היעדרות" (the write held with `page.route` until the page is left), a removal refused twice draws its alert anew and then closes, and the overlap's sheet closes with Escape; `clock-team.spec.ts`'s own shift and breaks close their sheet after a refusal (the breaks' before the save that follows, which would let go of a stuck hold); `clock-code.spec.ts` presses "חזרה לסריקה" in every task beside the Escape until the refusal is said, and finds it enabled then; `design.spec.ts`, a missing kind or days: its field has the focus, described by why, and no alert. Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`. Not run here: Playwright against the app, `pnpm build`.
- After CI (c6a2702): every new test passed but one, `absences.spec.ts` "a refusal on its way as her page is left by back…", at all three widths, on its own premise. It waited for the sheet to be hidden before letting the held write answer, but the write runs in a transition (`useSheetWrite`), and React 19 holds a transition started meanwhile, Next's navigation back, until that action ends: the address changes at once, the team's page is drawn only with the answer. So her page is hidden, with its sheet, in the drawing of the refusal, the very drawing in which its anchor was let go of and the sheet drawn anew before. The test now lets the write answer, then expects the sheet hidden, then goes forward; the app doesn't change.

## 2026-11-24 · chat · accessibility, part 9: waiter mode and the file's lock

- On chat/a11y-orders from origin/main 1837c80 (#250). The sweep's lists-me-file findings 48, 49, 52 and 53; the rest of that area is the sibling part or came with #243. No migration, no new package, no SQL, and the server actions answer as before. In `packages/ui`: TextArea takes `quietMessage`, as GroupedField does since part 2 (its message on screen but out of its description, nothing drawn differently; the gallery's TextArea is unchanged), with its unit test; and, after the check, `ignoreHeldEnter` in `@ui/focus-after`, #251's own, with its unit test.
- F48, moving the menu's categories and places (`/orders/menu/categories`, `/orders/menu/places`): `useRowMoves` (`app/(app)/orders/menu/row-moves.ts`) keeps a ref to each row's arrows, as the list's items do. A row moved down is put back in the page after the one it passed, which took the focus from its arrow; once the move is drawn the same arrow of the same row has it again, or the other one once the row reached an end (`arrowAfter`), and where it went is said politely after it through the announcer: `app.orders.places.movedTo` "{name} עכשיו בשורה {place} מתוך {count}" and `app.orders.categories.movedTo` "{name} עכשיו במקום {place} מתוך {count}" (English "{name} is now row {place} of {count}" and "{name} is now {place} of {count}"). The row's arrow takes back a focus lost to what the save draws after (its answer, a newer press's, a refused save putting the row back) until the person puts the focus or a press anywhere else; a focus put elsewhere never moves.
- F49, a draft line's allergy saved without words (`orders/draft-panel.tsx`): said once, as sign-in's fields say a refusal (`answerHeard` and `useQuietWhy` from `app/(auth)/step-results.ts`). From "שמירת ההערה" the field takes the focus once its error is drawn (a layout effect keyed on each such save, `arrived`), and the announcer says why politely after the field's own read; from a press that left the focus on the field, at once. Either way why is out of the field's description while the field keeps the focus (TextArea's `quietMessage`) and back in it once the focus leaves; each such save is said, the same one again too. No alert.
- F52, the holder's file (`team/[person]/file/open-watch.ts`, `file-open-watch.tsx`): a focus moved from one element to another (`focusin` with a `relatedTarget`: by the keyboard, or by a desktop screen reader that moves the system focus) is activity, as a press is; a focus the screen puts on the page from nowhere (given back once the file shows again, or once what had it went) is not, so it keeps nothing open by itself. A phone's screen reader (VoiceOver, TalkBack) moves only its own cursor and fires no `focusin`, so reading the file there is still no activity: the warning below is what keeps a reader's file open. A minute before this tab's 15 minutes end (`WARN_BEFORE_MS`) the watch says so (`warn(true)`), and the file shows a `role="alert"` line in the dock, "התיק יינעל בעוד דקה, כי לא הייתה בו פעילות.", with "להשאיר פתוח", which calls the watch's `stayOpen`: activity asked of the server at once, past the minute's wait (nothing more when its own press or key just asked), so a screen reader's press with no pointer and no key keeps it open too. It never takes the focus. When an answer moves the 15 minutes on (by it, by any activity, or another tab in use), the warning goes, and if it was seen "התיק נשאר פתוח." is said after the focus that was on "להשאיר פתוח" went back where it came from in the file, else to its first heading; a file that closes says nothing more and its locked card takes the focus as before. Words in `app.team.file.idle` (English "The file will lock in a minute, as there was no activity in it.", "Keep it open", "The file stays open."). The lock stays a privacy control: with nobody there the file locks at its 15 minutes as before, and the warning slides nothing.
- F53, the menu's search in the order screen (`orders/picker.tsx`): once what is typed settles (`SEARCH_SAID_AFTER_MS`, 600 ms, in `orders/search-said.ts`) the announcer says what it found, once for each settled search: `app.orders.picker.foundCount` "{count, plural, one {נמצאה תוצאה אחת} other {נמצאו # תוצאות}}" ("1 result found", "# results found"), or the nothing-found words the template already has ("אין מנה שמתחילה ב-…"). Nothing as the screen opens or the search is emptied; the same search typed again is said again. The nothing-found line is no `role="status"` any more, so it isn't said twice.

React 19 takes a ref off in the commit's mutation phase only when its function changed, gives the focus back in `resetAfterCommit` (`restoreSelection`, which also puts every ancestor's scroll back) and puts refs on in the layout phase, before the editor's own layout effect; `IconButton` hands the ref straight to its button, so with `arrowRefs` the moved row's arrow is on the list as React's focus reaches `away`. That test's table 10 sits above the band on the 390 by 844 page with rows below it to scroll, and its 4 px, margin included, leave the focus before the press moving nothing. (2) The file's `focusWhenDrawn` under the page's clock: Playwright's own frames fall on 16 ms marks, so the second try comes 16 to 32 ms after the drawing, and "התיק נשאר פתוח." waits for it; nothing on the first test's path is held, so its three lines are said at once, as before. - After the third check (five findings, all confirmed; two pairs of them the same gap): (1 and 5) a move is said by the saves that have it, not by whichever change answered last. A table moved down that saved, then a rename (or a removal, an add, a department) sent before its answer and refused, was said as "4 שוב בשורה 5 מתוך 12", back, in the row the move had put it in, the server's list. Each change now tells the moves its number with every answer, those a later change overtook too (`answered(change, saved, latest)`), the move is numbered by the change that carried it (`moving(..., change)`), and once the latest change answers the move is heard as moved when its own change or any later one saved (each was made from a list that has it), back only when none did (`moveAnswers` in `row-moves.ts`). (3 and 5) An answer a later change overtook no longer drives its caller: the places' and the categories' `change()` give `null` for it, as the later save decides, and the add, the removal and the undo act only on a true or a false. - After CI's third run (run 45573860861 on 88be8c7, which merged origin/main 257809c after 59636ae): (1) `orders-menu.spec.ts`, "a change refused while a later one was on its way leaves the screen to the later one", at 390, 820 and 1440 px: "4 עכשיו בשורה 5 מתוך 12" never said. The test was right and the app wrong. Next sets the router's state for each server action in a transition, React holds transitions on one state together until the latest is ready, and an action's own answer comes before its state. So the move's answer, saved, didn't draw its list while the rename was on its way, and the rename's refusal (`setOrder(null)`) showed the list from before both: table 4 back in row 4 for a moment, the move said there ("4 עכשיו בשורה 4"), and the move's list a few ms later. Reproduced in Chromium with the places editor through the React Compiler, Next's React, and Next's sequential action queue and `use(state)` modelled: before, "בשורה 4" and the old list drawn for 3 ms; now "בשורה 5", the list never going back. The places' and the categories' `change()` now keep each save that went through, overtaken ones too, with its ids, until the server's list is drawn anew, and a refusal shows that list, else the one drawn (`useLastSaved` and `lastSaved` in `orders/menu/saved-list.ts`). The console, which reads no version, keeps it the same way. The same reproduction runs the test's two later steps, which CI never reached (the removals, the add), and the older move test: all pass. (2) `file-a11y.spec.ts`, the twin's quarter of an hour ending on "להשאיר פתוח", at 820 px only: "פרטים מזהים" never focused. Not 59636ae: from ef7c6f8 to HEAD the only change on this path is `done`'s value and the kept line waiting for true. The focus calls are the same, `stop` ended the second try before as after, and the merge touched nothing on this path. The cause is older, found by reproduction (the real file view, gate and watch in the AppShell, the app's Tailwind compiled, Playwright's own clock, and less motion asked for, as playwright.config.ts does). The design system's reduced-motion net (`globals.css`, every element's transitions 0.01 ms, and an element's transition is of every property unless it names some) makes the file's `visibility` a transition as `invisible` goes, on its wrapper and on each title. That transition stays at its start, hidden, until a drawing of the page moves it on. So the title refused the focus on the drawing that shows it in every run logged, and the second try, two of the page clock's frames on (timers, not drawings), was refused too whenever no drawing came between: 3 of 126 runs, at all three widths, and 4 of 6 with the file held hidden 45 ms longer. Without less motion the first try lands. `focusWhenDrawn` now puts the focus back on each drawing from the second while it is still lost, until it lands or the person puts it elsewhere (at most `DRAWN_WITHIN_MS`, with a last try then), and `done` settles only then: 6 of 6 with the 45 ms. The test is unchanged, and its comment says why it failed. Tests: unit `saved-list.test.ts` (new: nothing saved, the latest save while the server's list isn't drawn, the latest of several, forgotten once drawn) and `file-focus.test.ts` (refused three times, it lands on the fourth drawing and only then settles; never landing, it tries on each drawing and once more at the end; put elsewhere meanwhile, it stays; stopped between drawings, false). e2e: comments in `orders-menu.spec.ts` and `file-a11y.spec.ts`, no assertion changed. Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`. Not run here: Playwright against the app, `pnpm build`. Left as it is: the reduced-motion net gives every element a 0.01 ms transition of every property, `visibility` included; only the file hides something and focuses into it on the drawing that shows it, so the net is left for a part of its own.
- After CI's fourth run (run 45589020390 on 88bf460): `orders-menu.spec.ts`, "a change refused while a later one was on its way leaves the screen to the later one", at 390 and 1440 px (820 passed): "4 עכשיו בשורה 5 מתוך 12" still never said. The test was right and the app wrong, and the third run's fix was right but not enough: its model resolved each save's promise before the next save went, and Next doesn't. In Next 16.4.6 the server action reducer (`server-action-reducer.js`) waits only for the answer's first row (`createFromFetch`), settles the caller's promise with the result `a`, and returns the router's state at once, so the action queue sends the next action then (`runRemainingActions` in `app-router-instance.js`). `a` is the action's result as a promise (`Promise.resolve(actionResult)` in `action-handler.js`), which React Flight writes as a row of its own after the first (`serializeThenable`, `pingTask`) and the client reads as a chunk that doesn't hold the first row back (`$@`). So the move's promise settles once that row is read, while the rename's request is already out, and its abort (`route.abort()`, a fetch that fails, its promise rejected at once by the reducer) can come back before it. The rename's refusal was the latest change's answer, so the move was heard by it alone, with its own save still to answer: as back ("4 שוב בשורה 4 מתוך 12" on the list from before both, or "...5" when Next drew the move's list first, untrue either way), and the move's answer, overtaken, said nothing after. Whichever comes first is a race, so 820 passed this time. Checked, and not the cause: the places and the categories draw one list at every width (the test reads 12 fields at all three), `useRowMoves` is used once in each, and nothing deduplicated or cleared the line; it was never queued with the right words. Reproduced in Chromium: Next's action queue, `callServer` and the reducer as next/dist has them, `useActionQueue`'s `useOptimistic` and `use`, the page's segment drawn as `layout-router.js` draws it (`Activity`, `useDeferredValue`, `use`), each save a real fetch that the runner's `page.route` holds, lets through or aborts, and the places and categories editors through the React Compiler with Next's React. With the result's row read with the first one, 88bf460 passes, as the earlier model did; read 20 or 80 ms after it (the abort came back a few ms after the first row), 88bf460 fails both of the test's checks there at every width, as CI did. Now: (1) a move is heard only once no change is on its way (`answered(change, saved, settled)`, the editors counting the changes out), whichever answers first; (2) it is said from a layout effect, on the drawing of the answer that settled the saves (heard is a state, so it is read with that drawing's rows), and only when that drawing shows what they did: the row on the side of the one it passed that the saves say (`moveFrom`, `moveSaid` in `row-moves.ts`), else on the first later drawing that does, and never once the screen went, a new move was pressed or a later change answered; (3) a refusal keeps the list of the newest change that saved, by its number (`lastSaved.saved(list, change)`), and a save that answers after the latest change's refusal shows its list, the server's then. So the words are only ever where the row is on screen and true of it. In the reproduction, at 390, 820 and 1440 px with the result's row at 0, 20 and 80 ms and the page's data at 0, 50 and 300 ms after it, both of the spec's tests about saves on their way pass every check, 27 runs of 27 (88bf460: the 9 with the row read with the first pass, the 18 with it later fail those two checks), and the categories' move with a rename refused says "עיקריות עכשיו במקום 3 מתוך 4" and never "שוב" (88bf460: back, with the row late). Tests: unit `row-moves.test.ts` (`moveFrom`: the row passed, none at an end; `moveSaid`: moved, back, a move up, counted after other changes, waiting while the drawing doesn't show what the saves did, by the saves once the row passed is gone, nothing once the row is; `moveAnswers`: the rename's refusal first and the move's save after, heard as moved only then; waiting for a change from before the move too) and `saved-list.test.ts` (the newest save kept when an older answer comes after it; a save answered after the server's list with it was drawn). e2e: `orders-menu.spec.ts` also checks that "4 שוב בשורה 4 מתוך 12" is never said, the words 88bf460 said in the reproduction, and its comment says why; no assertion weakened. Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check`. Not run here: Playwright against the app, `pnpm build`.
## 2026-11-25 · chat · availability: each day's suggested hours from the business's own shifts

- On chat/fill-suggest from origin/main 9c48b8a (#257). The plan's PR 3b, from the review of part 1: workers who kept the availability form's suggested hours were rarely placed by the fill, because those hours (09:00–17:00, then 18:00–23:00; Friday 08:00 until 15:00 or candle lighting; Saturday havdalah to 23:30) didn't cover whole shifts. No migration, no new package, no change in `packages/ui`. New words, from the review: a line in the day's sheet naming the day's shifts (`app.availability.shiftHours`) and a sentence more in the regular shifts' hint (`modules.scheduling.settings.shifts.hint`), both in both languages; the form's other words, its three shapes, checks and deadline are as they were. A visible change: every business with shifts sees its own hours in an empty day, and the day's sheet names each shift's hours.
- `@domain/availability-form`: `suggestedDay(day, shabbat, shifts)` is what an empty day opens with: from the earliest start to the latest end of the business's shifts that day (an end past midnight on the next day, as the ranges allow), each shift first cut where the form's Shabbat rules cut the day (Friday ends by candle lighting, Saturday starts from havdalah) and one wholly inside Shabbat left out. Hours kept as suggested so cover every shift that day that Shabbat leaves whole; a shift Shabbat cuts stays open in the fill with its time named, as before. Shifts around the clock (a day or more from the first start to the last end) give two ranges that meet at the last shift's start, since a range is shorter than a day; joined, they cover it. A day with no shift, or none Shabbat leaves, opens with the usual day as before (`usualRange`, today's code). `suggestedRange(day, rows, shabbat, shifts)` ("הוספת שעות") gives an empty day its first range, and a day with hours something that overlaps none of its rows whenever anything can, in this order: what the day's shifts still need (the part of the day's suggested hours the rows leave free, after them if any is, never one starting past midnight), the usual evening, the day's latest free hours until midnight inside Shabbat's limits and no longer than the usual ones (the restaurant's 09:00–23:30 gets 23:30–00:00), and only on a day with no free minute the usual hours all the same. `shiftHoursOn(day, shabbat, shifts)` is what the day's sheet lists: each of the day's shifts once, earliest first, in its own hours, one wholly inside Shabbat left out. `ShiftHours` (a pattern's days and hours, never its name or how many it needs) and `shiftHoursOf`. `@domain/staffing`'s `shiftsOn` is generic, so the form reads a day's shifts as the builder draws them.
- The source: `AvailabilityWeek.shiftHours`, from `weekContext`'s `settings.shifts` (the scheduling manifest's `shifts`, which the template fills and `/manage/scheduling` edits), the same patterns the builder draws a week from (`context.patterns`). `openDraft` takes the week's Shabbat and shift hours; the day sheet's "הוספת שעות" passes them. The twins: the availability twin's week has the restaurant template's hours (`demoShiftPatterns`), the shop's running-week twin the shop's.
- Tests: `availability-form.test.ts`: `shiftHoursOf`; `suggestedDay` (the templates' days, a break between shifts inside, only that weekday's shifts; Friday cut at candle lighting and Saturday at havdalah, each shift before spanning, summer's havdalah after 19:30 and Jerusalem's December candles before the café's 16:00; open on Shabbat as any day; the usual day with no shifts, with none on Saturday, and with every shift inside Shabbat; around the clock, more than a day, and a minute short of one); 63 sets of shifts (the three templates and 60 generated), each closed on Shabbat three ways and open, every day: hours the form takes, at most two ranges, covering each shift whole exactly when Shabbat doesn't cut it, and the second range valid on its own; `suggestedRange` (the old cases with no shifts, an empty day's first range, each step of its order with its own cases, the sheet's own steps for each template every day, and in the 63 sets the added hours beside the day's as it opens wherever there is room, `roomBeside`); `shiftHoursOn` (each shift once, earliest first, in its own hours where Shabbat cuts what can be marked, one wholly inside Shabbat left out). `fill-week.test.ts`: the test that showed the gap now shows, per template (restaurant, café, shop) with and without Shabbat, and for day-and-night shifts, every shift covered by every kept answer, which the form would send as it is; ten people fill every place (44, 37, 23, 14), and three leave places open only where everyone off the shift covers it and a rule stopped them. A new one: kept answers leave open only what Shabbat cuts (the restaurant's and the café's Saturday night in summer, `shabbat` with havdalah; the café's Friday in Jerusalem's December, with candle lighting), everything else filled.
