- After the check (four findings): the update section's "מתי" names the line that says what waits for release, "מחכה לשחרור לעסקים", in the agent's note and in each session's summary, the Sunday one too, with "כולל שינוי במבנה" for a migration and a `בטל` of something released reaching the businesses only through a release (`docs/AGENT.md`'s side of it is the live session's). A check after a release that fails has a first step: a build that failed leaves the site as it was, so nothing is touched; one that is `Ready` but doesn't answer or sign in is brought back with Vercel's `Instant Rollback`, which returns that version with its values but not the schema, and holds the next release until `Undo Rollback`; either way a session is opened at once. The first release's check opens a session and brings nothing back, since no business is on the site yet. The update section offers a look at the demo's site, already on that code, before releasing. B3's apply says its two lines are never run, and step B's rerun follows the update section's steps 1 and 2, expecting only what came since B3, or nothing. Every appendix row that names Vercel ends with a `Redeploy` of the current Production deployment, from the branch `production`, never the top one (usually a preview of main from step 4); the cron secret goes to Vault only once that deployment is `Ready`, and the old secret key is deleted only then, after a sign-in; B9 says the same. `tooling/production-doc.test.ts` holds each.
- Turnstile is the next PR (P9b): four places send codes (sign-in, join, the hiring file's bank code, the holder's step-up), each needing a token once CAPTCHA is on.
- Defaults applied (to ask Orel), as recommended: `/enter` on the closed site goes to `/sign-in`, not "not found"; no link to the demo from the real site's sign-in; the demo's site keeps phone sign-in for the team's tests; a value that is neither fails the build; `/health` asks the server only, not the database, at `/health`, not `/api/health`; one job, one approval a run, "apply" off is the dry run; the run stops on a project with demo businesses; Orel alone approves, without Prevent self-review; Turnstile in its own PR right after; the closed site served in CI (about 15 seconds); the design gallery stays on the real site; the instruction files are the live session's.
- Next: P9b, Turnstile.

## 2026-11-22 · chat · bigger teams, the first fixes (#1)

- On chat/scale-fixes from origin/main 9af447f (H6a #226 merged). Orel's decision of 22 November on #1: after H6b, the real-business layer and generic scale, for any business of a few hundred people. Three parts: a document to everyone reaches everyone; every read that can pass PostgREST's 1,000 rows pages or says why it can't; a business is told plainly when its space for files is filling. Out, as asked: the hiring file, the schedule's caps, waiter mode and anything that costs.
- Migration `20261127000100_bigger_teams.sql`: `public.send_document`, the newest body (20261111000700_documents.sql) with only `> 200` and `'One to 200 people'` made 1000 (the recipients were already one insert and their lines one `private.notify`); a new `public.file_space(actor, business)`, STABLE, the secret key's alone, for an active member who sees the management page (`manage.view`) or sends documents (`private.sends_documents`), 42501 for anyone else, giving sizes only: the documents' bytes as their refusal counts them (`private.documents_held_bytes`), the chat files' as the console counts them (Storage objects under the business's folder), both spaces as the team set them, and whether it takes files; and `public.admin_storage`, the newest body (20261111000500_chat_files.sql) with each business's `document_bytes`. No table, no policy. Its line in database.types.ts by hand.
- Part 1: `DOCUMENT_RECIPIENTS_MAX` is 1000. `audienceOf` no longer cuts at 200 (past it the rest went by id order and the count said 200); `audienceFits` says none, fits or too many. Past 1,000 the send screen's line says how many were chosen and the most one document takes, in the danger colour, with "send it to each department in turn" (or "split the team into departments" where there are none), and pressing send says "צריך לבחור עד 1,000 אנשים" before any file goes up. `tooling/documents.test.ts` also checks that a page's photo and 1,000 ids fit Next's default 1 MB for a server action.
- Part 2: `@db/pages` gained `IDS_PER_READ` and `inParts` (moved from `documents.ts`), `readAllPages` (a read's own answer, so the missing-table and missing-column fallbacks stay) and `allPagesIn`/`readAllPagesIn` (ids in parts of 100, each part page by page). Paged now, each ordered by its key last: hourly wages, corrections, entries to confirm, the clock's and pay's people, member names (in parts when for some), employment periods and payroll details, the chat directory and authors, open invites, the team's members and numbers (`team_phones` too), the access screen's and the roles' people, departments' people (and the console's), a week's availability, a list's people, the lists' summaries' four reads, open and closed lists, fixable lists, lists by window, readings, a week's number items, templates' items and times and named roles, the template lists, and the roles' templates and open invites. 29 reads that can't pass the cap say why beside them (`// bounded:`: one page of the log or the bell, one list, one template, one person's businesses, the schedule's 200 a week). `tooling/bounded-reads.test.ts` fails on a new read of a growing table in packages/db that does neither; the hiring file and taking orders' files are named there as kept out (known issue 83).

The office and a sender see a flat card on `/chat/documents` (above every shape) and on the send screen when the space for documents is filling (80%) or full (less than a page's photo left). A worker sending to the business learns of a full space only from the refusal, as before.

Another tab's activity starts a tab's 15 minutes again and asks the server too, at most once a minute, telling nobody back, so a file whose step-up is gone (signed out in another window, or another person's or business's now) closes as the server says (`closed`: nothing told, nothing locked) instead of staying up while someone works in another window. A screen Next shows again is never closed by its own clock, which heard nothing while it was hidden: it is held before it is painted and the server asked, whatever its own last activity, shown on an open answer and closed as `closed` on a closed one, so pressing Back to an old file no longer locks the device's files in every tab. An answer that doesn't come within 10 seconds (`KEEP_OPEN_WAIT_MS`) closes the file as one that failed would, and while the file is held for longer than 300 ms a quiet line with a spinner says "בודקים שהתיק עדיין פתוח" in its place (`app.team.file.checking`, a `role="status"` always there), so it never just vanishes. Signing out (`SignOutForm`, now `signOutAndLeave` in `app/sign-out.ts`, the station's sign-out too) and locking a device as a station (`LockStation`, not on /design's twins) close every open file on the device at once (`closeFilesOnDevice`), before the page goes. Tests: unit for the watch (6, in place of 2, and 3 changed: no answer, no answer on coming back, shown again at 14 minutes, shown again past its own 15 minutes answered open and closed, another tab's activity asking once a minute, and closed by the server on it), `closeFilesOnDevice` (2) and `signOutAndLeave` (4). Checked here: `pnpm check`. Not run here: Playwright and `pnpm build`. Default applied (to ask Orel): signing out on /design's station twin also closes the device's real open files, since the form can't tell the twin's stand-in from a real sign-out.
- After the final check (three findings, each confirmed: an idle tab's failed check on another tab's activity locked the tab in use; another tab's word extended a tab's 15 minutes without asking, so a file whose step-up another business's had replaced stayed on screen; a tab back from a phone's freeze judged "idle" by a clock that had heard nothing, and locked the device), a simpler rule, numbered the same in `./team/[person]/file/open-watch.ts`. 1. Only the server says whether the files are open on this device: the step-up cookie, slid only by a check for the person's activity in a tab (point 5) and gone by itself 15 minutes after the last. 2. Only a deliberate act closes them on the whole device: "נעילת התיק" or signing out, which tell every tab `closed` and forget the cookie, as before. 3. Anything else closes the file in its own tab only, telling no tab and forgetting nothing: the server answering closed, a check that fails or gets no answer within 10 seconds, another tab's lock heard (which no longer locks again). A tab's own 15 minutes passing, a page coming back (past them too, after a freeze) and a screen Next shows again close nothing by themselves: the file is hidden and the server asked once; open shows it again (the person is busy in another tab), closed or failed closes it in that tab. `closeAndDraw` tells and locks for `pressed` only, in place of `lockAndDraw`, and the `idle` reason is gone. 4. A tab's 15 minutes end when the server's do: its own check, answered open, says how long the step-up has left (15 minutes once slid, never more), counted from the answer, which comes after the server's moment and the cookie's, so when they pass the server finds the step-up gone unless activity in some tab slid it since. A press, a key and another tab's word ask the server at most once a minute, one within the minute kept for its end rather than dropped; the word alone extends nothing. 5. Only a check for the person's activity in a tab (the file opening, a press or a key, coming back, shown again by Next) slides the step-up, and only it, answered open, tells the other tabs `active`; a check for the tab's own 15 minutes or for another tab's word only reads the step-up and tells nothing. So tabs left alone never keep each other open: the files close on the device 15 minutes after the last activity in any tab, a minute more at most for a press kept for the minute's end, which slides only while fresh (`PRESS_FRESH_MS`, the minute and 5 seconds): run long after in a tab frozen in the background, its check only reads (a check of that fix found it; a unit test fails without it). 6. While a file is held for an answer, "בודקים שהתיק עדיין פתוח" after 300 ms and the 10-second give-up, which closes that tab only. A message carries its kind alone now (`active` lost its moment; an older build's is left), and `OpenFileMemory` keeps only whether the screen showed the file. The focus and its announcements stay as in 556f129. Tests: the watch's unit tests rewritten on a device of tabs and a stand-in for the server's step-up, one per point and the three findings' cases (an idle tab's failed check never locks the tab in use, twice: a failed action and a lost answer; a replaced step-up is never extended by heard activity; a tab resumed after a freeze never locks the device, with its timer, its coming back or the words first), five of the screen's own, `closeAndDraw` (3), and the tabs' messages by kind; e2e: the idle test passes the page's 15 minutes first (the server says open, the file shows again, the cookie kept), then clears the cookie for the server's; the two-tab lock is unchanged. Checked here: `pnpm check`. Not run here: Playwright and `pnpm build`. Default applied (to ask Orel): a tab that closes on its own while the device's step-up is still fresh (a check lost on a weak network) shows "התיק נעול" and asks for a code, though reloading it would show the file.
- After the check of those fixes (one finding, confirmed: every check slid the step-up, a tab's own 15 minutes and a word heard included, so two idle tabs whose checks came under 15 minutes apart kept each other's file open for good, with nobody at the device, and "הצגה" kept working for a passer-by): point 5 above, and point 4's time left. `keepFileOpenAction(business, slide)` takes the slide as a zod-parsed boolean, refusing anything else, under the same gate (`fileHolder`) and the same no-store answer of a POST: `true` slides as before (`keepFileOpen`) and answers 15 minutes left; `false` only reads (`fileOpenLeft` in `step-up.ts`, which `fileOpenFor` now uses, never sealing the cookie again) and answers the step-up's time left (`stepUpLeft` in `@domain/file-holders`: 15 minutes less its age, never more than 15). `KeepOpenAnswer` is `{ open: true, leftMs }` or `{ open: false }`; the watch slides only for its tells, sets its 15 minutes to the answer's moment plus the time left, and closes the file (`failed`) on a time left that isn't one; the twin answers 15 minutes. Tests: unit for `stepUpLeft`, the action (both ways under each gate, a read that slides nothing and says the time left, a slide that isn't a boolean refused), and the watch (points 1, 4 and 5 changed to the time left and to reads that slide nothing; tabs left alone: two idle tabs 30 seconds apart, hearing each other or not, both closed within 16 minutes of the last press with the step-up over 15 minutes old; a check for the clock or a word never slides; a lone tab still closes 15 minutes after its last press; the tab in use keeps itself and the idle tab beside it open; at most 15 minutes from any answer, and a time left that isn't one closes); the three findings' cases unchanged. The e2e idle test changes only in its comment: the page's 15 minutes ask only to read, the server says open, and the second 15 minutes, past the time it said, find the cookie cleared. Checked here: `pnpm check`. Not run here: Playwright and `pnpm build`.
- Next: H6b.

Outside the app's frame for everyone, someone in another business too, since the `(app)` layout draws the screen with no business in place of every page and isn't drawn again on a client navigation.
- The gate (`app/(account)/me/past/past-file.ts`), in order: the id a uuid (any case); someone signed in; the business among their departures (`currentDepartures`, now a per-request `cache` in `app/(app)/departures.ts`, which `currentDeparture` reads too); a view session refused before any file is read; then `getEmployeeFile` for the session's own person. Off says so, nothing of theirs says so, no keys says "not available yet", anything else is not found (the root's), the same for each. - The screen: read-only, no main action and no write, in the business's colour, in Hebrew or in English if the profile chose English. Who they are (the ID or document masked with "הצגה", a passport's country and expiry, the names, the birth date), the address and the ways to reach them (titled as step 2 is), the account (bank and branch, whose, the number masked), the pension as answered, each year's Form 101, and "מה נשמר אצל {business}". A part a holder handed on says so; a part with nothing says "לא נמסר". Phone one column; tablet two (who they are and how to reach them | the rest); desktop the same, the first wider. Its other states (`off`, `empty`, `viewing`, `unavailable`) in the same frame with one way back.
- The ways in: `PastJobsSection` ("עבודות קודמות", "התיק שלי ב{business}", "בצוות עד {month}" in Israel's zone) on `/me` after "העסקים שלי", and on `NoBusinessScreen` (new `past` prop: the list, then the way out), only for a business with the module on and something of theirs in the file, each read once.
- The words promise what the code keeps: the details stay with the business after leaving, they can be seen here while the business uses the file, managers no longer see the emergency contact (`team_emergency_contacts` joins active members), and the business is the one to ask for a fix or a deletion. No length of time is said: nothing deletes a file yet (H8).
- Twins: `/design/screens/past-file` (`?state=passport|returned|off|empty|viewing|unavailable`, `?lang=en`, `?from=none`), `?past=1` on `/design/shell/me` and `/design/screens/no-business`, and a gallery link. Tests: unit for the gate, the list, the action, the parts and the drawn screen in both languages; `employee-files-guard.test.ts` (no cache, no value in a log or a URL) and `proxy.test.ts`; e2e `employee-file-past.spec.ts` (five tests at every width: the main story with nothing logged, no-store and not found for a business never joined and for Noa, English, the module off, the profile's way in), the screens and the design walks.

## 2026-11-22 · chat · sheets rise above the phone keyboard

- On chat/sheet-keyboard from origin/main 7d2f693. Orel's report of 22 November, on his phone: a small sheet with a field to type in opens the keyboard, and the keyboard covers the sheet. The cause: `Sheet` is `position: fixed` at the foot of the layout viewport, which iOS never shrinks for the keyboard and Android Chrome by default doesn't either (it shrinks only the visual viewport).
- `packages/ui/use-keyboard-lift.ts`: `keyboardLift` (pure) reads the two viewports, `layoutHeight - (visualViewport.height + offsetTop)`, never below 0, smaller as iOS moves the visual viewport down to a field, nothing under 40px, and nothing for a pinch (`scale` not 1); `watchKeyboard` follows the visual viewport's resize and scroll and the window's resize (turning the phone), says only changes, and once it has stood still 250ms with a keyboard up brings the field with the focus into sight within the sheet's body (`keepFocusInView`, `nearestScroll`: `block: "nearest"` by hand, since `scrollIntoView` would also move the page behind and the visual viewport); `useKeyboardLift` runs it while the sheet's scrim is drawn, the scrim's height being the layout viewport's (`innerHeight` follows the visual viewport on some browsers). No visualViewport, nothing lifted.
- `Sheet`: `--sheet-bottom` (8px over the keyboard) and `--sheet-max` (what is on screen, 8px from the top and its safe area) in its style, read by its classes with the old place as their fallback, so with no keyboard (a tablet, a desktop) nothing changes. `bottom` and `max-height` ease over 200ms, none with reduced motion; the drag and the way in and out keep the transform. Every sheet has it, ShareSheet, AttachSheet and the rest through `Sheet`. No `interactive-widget` in the viewport meta.
- Not changed: the chat's `Composer` is sticky in the conversation's own page, not fixed over a locked one, so the browser's own focus scroll takes it up as any field on a page, and no one has seen it covered; `FloatingDock` and the availability form's dock hold buttons, the tab bar links and the skeleton its placeholders, no field; `ChoiceMenu` is placed by Radix within the screen and has no field.
- Tests: unit (`use-keyboard-lift.test.ts`: iOS, Android, no keyboard, a browser that shrinks the layout viewport, iOS moving the visual viewport, a pinch, turning, rounding, the keyboard closing, the settle and the stop, no visualViewport, the field kept in sight; `sheet.test.ts`: the sheet drawn carries the lift, and nothing without it). design.spec.ts: "a sheet stands over the phone's keyboard (faked)", the phone only, `fakeKeyboard` shrinking `visualViewport.height` and sending its resize, since Playwright opens no keyboard.
- Checked here: `pnpm check`, and the dev server's /design/chat at 375px with the same faked keyboard (the sheet 8px over it and 8px under the top, its field in sight, moved by iOS's offset, nothing for a pinch, back down once closed, the field brought back into the body after the settle, the page behind not scrolled) and at the desktop's width (unchanged). Not run here: `pnpm build`, Playwright.
- Only a real phone can confirm: iOS Safari and the installed app, Android Chrome; that the lift follows the keyboard without a jump; iOS moving the visual viewport as well; and the chat's composer.
- Defaults applied: the gap over the keyboard 8px, as over the safe area; under a pinch nothing is lifted; the composer left as it is.
- After review: (1) a sheet opened from a button while a field on the page kept the keyboard (the chat's attach button on iOS) flew up to a keyboard about to close, then dropped about 300px during its opening spring. `watchKeyboard` now takes `within` (the sheet's Content, passed to `useKeyboardLift` as the scrim is) and lifts only while a field to type in inside it holds the focus (`opensKeyboard`: an input that is not a button, box, file and the like, a textarea or contenteditable, not read-only), read again on `focusin` and on `focusout` by its `relatedTarget`, so going from one field to the next is no drop; a field that autofocuses (CodeSlots) is inside at once and lifts. (2) Sideways, with some 130px over the keyboard, the header and the `shrink-0` footer left the body no height: the field vanished and the footer went under the keyboard. Below `SHEET_FRAME_MIN` (220px: header, a footer with a large button, one field) `sheetScrollsWhole` makes the Content scroll whole (`overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain`, the body `shrink-0`), and the settle brings the field into sight in the body, then in the sheet (`keepFocusInSheet`). (3) `nearestScroll`'s comment and test now say what it does: a field taller than the view aligns its top when below and its bottom when above (CSSOM). Unit tests for each; checked on the dev server with the faked keyboard: the attach sheet opened from the focused composer is never lifted, the close button or a radio taking the focus drops it, and at 844x360 with 130px left the new message sheet and the choices sheet (its quantity in the footer) scroll whole, the field 12px over the sheet's foot.

- Settings: eleven fields in `modules/employee_files/manifest.ts`, each `level: "owner"`: `askBank` and `askAddress` (on), `askEmergencyContact` and `askWorkLanguage` (off), `form101` (`print`, `link`, `off`), `form101Provider`, `form101Link` (https to a named host, its scheme and host kept in lower case), `payrollProvider`, `employerName`, `employerPhone` (a mobile or a landline, kept as E.164) and `deductionsFile` (nine digits from 9). `homeWidget` is `{ minRole: "worker" }`. `@domain/settings-form` gains the meta `entry` (`link`, `phone`, `digits`): `/manage` and the console draw such a text setting left to right with its keyboard, a phone as people write it, and the activity log says it left to right and "" as "לא נקבע".
- Migration `20261126000400_employee_files_settings.sql`: `private.employee_files_settings` (the manifest's defaults under what the business stored) and `private.file_asks`; `private.file_groups` replaced so `askAddress` and `askBank` decide the groups the file counts (a stored value that isn't a boolean reads as the default), so `file_missing`, the worker's read, the status and the export follow at once; `public.set_module_settings` replaced (20261120000200_order_taking_takers.sql's newest body, one branch after taking orders'): every field an owner's and the team's (42501), no key outside the eleven, each value one the manifest outputs (22023, `private.file_setting_line` and `private.file_setting_link`). Both keep their signatures; revoked and granted again all the same. `database.types.ts` doesn't change (no public signature does). The app works before it reaches a project: everything reads as asked, and a setting saves without the database's checks (the manifest's still apply; the module is off on the hosted project).
- The flow: `@domain/employee-file-settings` (the asks, `fileGroupsAsked`, `form101SetupOf`, the notice's names, `bankChangeToTell`, the seen cookie), `@domain/phone`'s `normalizeIsraeliPhone` and `displayIsraeliPhone`, and the steps' helpers taking the steps on screen (`fileParts`, `fileStepsOn`) with a state `optional`. The address and bank steps go when not asked, unless the file holds them (then said, and they can be changed or emptied); step 2 is "דרכי קשר" without the address, and takes the work language and the emergency contact (saved after the details by `saveEmergencyContact`, only when changed); step 5 by link shows "מילוי הטופס אצל {provider}" (`noopener noreferrer`, the site it opens under it) and "מסרתי את הטופס" marks it with the settings' route, decided on the server; with Form 101 off the step is gone and the done screen sends the file. - Today: the accent card "התיק שלי ב…" while something is missing (not for owners; flat when the first steps or an open mode hold the accent), else the quiet row says "הפרטים בתיק הושלמו"; a bank-change card for 14 days after `bank_changed_at`, with "לא שיניתי" (`disputeBankChangeAction`, then the card's second state and "הבנתי") and "אני שיניתי", kept per device in `ca_bank_seen` by the database's moment, read again on the server (`seenBankChangeAction`), forgotten on signing out. `currentFileToday` never takes Today down.
- `/join`: after the claim, the business's modules are read with the secret key (`getBusinessModulesForTeam`), and a worker or a manager lands on `/me/file` (the welcome) while the module is on (`landingAfterJoin`); owners and everyone else on `/home`.

## 2026-11-21 · chat · taking orders: offline (waiter mode P7)

- On chat/order-offline from origin/main e5a0d06 (P6's branch, PR #213, on top of main e988dd8 with P1 to P5 merged). Orel's decisions of 20 November: every phase, §16's recommendations. No migration: `database.types.ts` unchanged, no pgTAP file, no Migrate run.
- The outbox (`app/(app)/orders/outbox.ts`): one per page, outside React, the only writer of the drafts, the rounds on their way, their refusals and the menu kept on the phone. The mode's provider reads it through `useSyncExternalStore`, so a mode hidden in an `<Activity>` or drawn afresh reads the same, and P4's `claim`/`keep` is gone. `OrdersOutbox` in the `(app)` layout starts it for whoever takes orders, so a killed app sends what waited as it opens, on any screen. A round goes at once and again at 1, 2, 5, 10 and 30 s, woken by `online` and the page shown, with one last keepalive as it hides (64 KB in all until shown again, once each); it says "עוד לא נשלח" only once its strict write is done, and the screen's send lets go then, so another may follow offline. After 10 minutes it checks one's own rounds (`order_mine`, whose ids are the keys) and asks: "שליחה" (again after another 10) or "הסרה מהטלפון" with "ביטול". A refusal goes back to its draft's front with its reason in one transaction with the settle, once across tabs. 401 and `notMember` take the person's rounds and menu off the phone (all businesses, or that one), drafts kept, with a one-time notice of place names in sessionStorage (`orders-lost`) on the app's banner, the door and the no-business screen; 409 `person` holds them in the page and draws the frame again.
- The store (`orders-db.ts`): IndexedDB `conceptapp-orders` v1, `rounds`, `drafts` (with the refusal and whether its screen showed it) and `menus`, each record with its person and business, read back through `@domain/orders-outbox`'s schemas; other tabs hear through `BroadcastChannel`; `memoryOrdersDb` where IndexedDB is missing or blocked, and in the tests. Another person's rounds, and any draft or menu, go after 16 hours untouched; one's own never silently.
- The server: `sendBody` gains a required `user`; `/orders-send` refuses another than the session's (409 `person`) before `viewOnly`, and a business not among `getOwnPlace`'s new `memberOf` (403 `notMember`), both before the database.
- Session ends (`orders-storage.ts`, which imports only the store): signing out asks once when an order of the person's hasn't gone ("יציאה ומחיקה" / "חזרה"), then clears their records in every business and what expired, awaited before the page goes; locking the clock station clears everyone's, after a warning line with how many and where. Both remove P4's `orders:` sessionStorage entries, which the outbox's first open also drops.
- Screens: a round on its way is "בשליחה", "עוד לא נשלח" or the question (`RoundState`, also over the items on a phone); the phone opens on the order when a round is on its way; the floor's tile "לא נשלח" (`PlaceTile` tone `unsent`), first on the status line, in the desktop table and the order's list of places, and the new takeaway's tile while its first round waits; a count on "שולחנות"; "אין חיבור" (`ConnectionNote`, §7.4's words) under the title of the floor, the order, "ההזמנות שלי", the prep screen and the till; outside the mode a banner above the screen (`OutboxBannerView`, never a toast) with "פתיחה", and each round that lands said to screen readers. The menu is kept on the phone and seeds the mode at once.
- The sync's pace (`ordersSyncPace`): 5 s while a round of one's own of an open order has a ticket at its department with lines left (doubling to 60), and on the prep screen (doubling to 30); else 15.
- Pure: `@domain/orders-outbox` (`rowTurn`, `askAge`, `sendVerdict`, `keepsRecord`, `sentAlready`, the schemas), `ordersSyncPace`, `nextOrdersSyncDelay`'s `pace`. The twin: states `asking`, `offline` and `lost`, its sender's `answer`/`remove`/`unsent`, five index rows; the gallery's "לא נשלח" tile.
- Tests: unit for the domain, the sync's pace and body, the route (`user` required, `person`, `notMember`), `getOwnPlace`'s `memberOf`, the store in memory, the engine (`outbox.test.ts`: kept before waiting, offline then online once, the retry waits, a refusal back and kept, another tab's settle, the question, `answer`, `remove`/`restore`, a round that went already, another person, a session switch, `person`, viewing, lost by 401 and by `notMember`, the hide's keepalive and its budget, expiry, another tab's message, a change before the first read, the menu, no store), the session ends, the send store's new fields, the twin, and a toast case; e2e `orders-offline.spec.ts` (five flows) with the shared helpers moved to `e2e/orders-parts.ts`, `orders.spec.ts`'s direct sends with `user` and the new refusals, `e2e/db.ts` `demoRoundSenders`, design.spec five URLs and a walk. ## 2026-11-21 · chat · taking orders: fixes and the till, on screen (waiter mode P5)

- On chat/order-fix-till from P4's branch (49c5817, #210, over main b0293d1). Orel's decisions of 20 November: every phase, §16's recommendations; P3's defaults he accepted (a manager off a shift fixes orders, a cancelled order stays final, a takeaway doesn't move, cancel and comp undone while the order is open, the log shows the unit price only).
- Migration `20261122000100_order_taking_fixes.sql`: the restaurant's and the café's 17 sentences only ("ביטול המנה", "סגירת השולחן", "העברה לשולחן אחר", "לפי מנה" and the rest), because only a migration writes a template's words. No function, table, grant or type changes: every write and its undo is P3's, today's closed orders and fixes are P4's `order_floor`. `database.types.ts` unchanged.
- The order screen: each sent line keeps P4's "החזרה מהשליחה" and gains "תיקון" (an IconButton named "תיקון: {name}"), which opens the line's sheet: one tap on a reason cancels ("ביטול הפריט", restaurant "ביטול המנה"), or gives it on the house for those who run orders; a cancelled line is put back and one on the house taken off it while the order is open; a taker on a ticket ready reads why the reasons are dimmed. One held write per screen holds every line fix and a whole order's cancel 8 seconds with "ביטול" on the mode's toast. "ההזמנה כולה" after what was sent: "סגירת ההזמנה" (restaurant "סגירת השולחן") written at once, its "ביטול" opening it again, refused while a held block waits and saying what isn't served yet; "העברה למקום אחר" to a free place, written at once, the phone's draft going with it and back on "ביטול"; "ביטול ההזמנה" for those who run orders, saying how many items go and that it's final. Under the title, where it moved from and when it opened again. A takeaway closed or cancelled while on screen says so, with its till.
- The till, `/orders/till/[order]`: by item and by round (a Segmented on a phone and a tablet, side by side on a desktop), the note that it is no receipt, no total, a line on the house marked with its price struck; close for an open order, "פתיחה מחדש" for a closed one, refused with its reason while the place is taken or the day is over; a cancelled order says who cancelled it and why, with no list. Drawn from the order whole (`orderTill`), so it stays current with the sync. A closed takeaway's page goes to its till. The places' tab stays current on it (the manifest's `also`).
- The floor: "נסגרו היום" under the places, each row to its till, the newest five and "הצגת כל N" on a phone and a tablet, a table of all on a desktop. `/manage/order_taking`: the owner's line, "היום: 3 החזרות, 2 ביטולים, 1 על חשבון הבית", to the activity log, no shekels, through a `summary` on `ModuleSettingsView`.
- Pure: `orderTill`, `lineStateOf` (@domain/orders); `lineFixes`, `closeState`, `reopenState`, `freePlaces`, `closedToday` (@domain/order-screens); `storeDraftMoved` (send-store.ts, `DraftStore.move`). Six server actions (cancel line, comp line, cancel order, move, close, reopen), parsed with the reasons' lists, a reason with every fix and none with its undo. `fixFailureWords` for their refusals. UI: `OrderLine mark`, `TillRow free`, both on /design.
- The twins: the store learns cancel, comp, a whole order's cancel, move, close by the order and reopen (refused as the database refuses); `real-orders.ts` reads them all; S0's till screen is gone, the twin draws the app's `TillView` (the station keeps S0's until P8). New states `fixed` and `reopenTaken`, and takeaway 2 cancelled in the evening's closed.
- Tests: unit for the domain, the actions, the refusals' words, the draft's move, the twin's fixes and the template words; `modules/order_taking/till.db.test.ts` (the phones' till and `order_till` alike, a round sent, cancelled and comped as the owner); pgTAP `order_taking_fixes.test.sql` (3); e2e `orders.spec.ts` five flows and `e2e/db.ts` helpers; five design walks, two updated, six URLs for axe. Checked here: `pnpm check`. Not run here: pgTAP, `pnpm test:db`, Playwright: first in CI.

  - The desktop's "נסגרו היום" names each row's button by its place, state and time (`floor.closed.rowLabel`), so two orders at one table read apart.
  - A serve or a "להוציא" sends a fix held on the order first, as close and move do: a taker's cancel on a new ticket never lands after a serve that makes it too late. e2e: Yoav serves the bar's ticket while his beer's cancel waits.
  - e2e: the till shows no 60.00 for table 4 (32 + 28), the floor reads table 4 open again after the reopen, "נסגרו היום" lists takeaway 1 as cancelled, and the owner's price and reason are read on the newest cancel's own row.
- After the second review:
  - The close's "ביטול" takes the close back whole: `public.undo_close_order` in 20261122000100 (no longer words only) opens the order again and, in one transaction, puts the tickets the close served (its `closed_at` and `closed_by`) back to new or ready; only by whoever closed it, within 2 minutes (`ORDER_RULES.closeUndoMinutes`, `closeUndoState`; 'window' otherwise, `failed.undoClose`), else reopen's refusals. pgTAP `order_taking_fixes.test.sql` 13, `database.types.ts`, `undoCloseOrder`, `undoCloseOrderAction`, on the order screen's and the till's toast and the twin (its store keeps `closeServed`). e2e: table 4's kitchen ticket is new again after the undo.
  - e2e: the till walk puts table 4's seeded order on today's business day first (`demoOrderToToday`), so a reset just after 05:00 no longer leaves it on yesterday's.
  - Close, move and cancel-order wait while a round of the screen is on its way, and say so (`order.actions.waitSend`): a close no longer serves a round the kitchen never saw, nor a move strands one at the old table.
  - The order's sheets close when its open order changes (closed or moved elsewhere, from another phone too), so none opens again over the next order at that place.
- After the third review:
  - The till's close waits while a round of the order is on its way and says so (`order.actions.waitSend`, `orderDraftKey`); the app's close and move go after every write sent before them (`inTurn`), so a line's fix sent on leaving the order's screen lands first. Both unit-tested; "לקופה" stays a link, as the till waits on its own.
  - The send and a refusal's way to send again wait while a close or a move is on its way (`sendLocked`), so the race no longer happens the other way round.
- After the fourth review:
  - The move's "ביטול" waits while a round sent at the new place is on its way: the mode's `whole` refuses it ("sending") and the toast offers it again (`toast.undoWaits`); once it goes, everything the new place's draft holds but what it held before goes back, and a refusal goes with its lines (`storeDraftMoved` `staying`, `refusalMoved`, unit-tested).
  - Writes of an order whole (close, move, both "ביטול"s, reopen, a whole order's cancel once it goes) are kept on their way in the mode's provider (`whole`, `wholeOnWay`, whole-on-way.ts, unit-tested), so the till's close, a move back or an undone close locks the send, a refusal's send again and the till on every screen of that order or place; and none goes while a round of its drafts is on its way.
  - While one is on its way, the rounds' take-back, "תיקון", "הוגש" and "להוציא" are disabled and nothing is held, and an open sheet closes (`Rounds waits`), so no fix lands after the close and its refusal never takes the close's "ביטול" away.
- After the fifth review: a write of an order whole never goes behind another of the same order, or of a draft it names, not answered yet (`wholeRefusal`, "writing", `order.actions.waitWrite`), so an older "ביטול" pressed while a move on or the till's close is on its way says so instead of undoing it in part; a "ביטול" that waits is offered again, or says why, as it is pressed (`OrdersModeValue.wholeRefusal`), so the news behind the toast is never told and swallowed in the same moment, and the till's button no longer flickers; a written close or move stays on its way until a read the mode started after its answer has landed (`readAfter`, the sync's `started`), so the close row, the rounds' buttons and the till's close wait while the screen still shows the order open; and the move's "ביטול" leaves the new place only its own: what its draft held as the order came is kept in memory by the store (`own`, never in the tab, `storeKept`), with its refusal (`ownRefusals`), more of one of its lines than it held goes back as a line of its own, and a line of its own that went out in one of the order's rounds is the order's (`storeSent`, `storeMovedBack`, `refusalMoved` with `own`, `DraftStore.moveBack`; unit-tested, the twin alike).

- CI's first e2e run (d831d80): the cancel and the comp flows timed out on the phone and the desktop, waiting on table 9's tile after "ההזמנות שלי". The tile's name also matches that screen's "שולחן 9 · בר", and Next keeps a screen left in a hidden `<Activity>`, so the click held on to a link that never showed again. `toFloor` in `e2e/orders.spec.ts` now waits for the floor's URL and heading before any tile is looked for, at every way back to the floor.

The sync's question is pure now (`nextOrdersSyncQuery`): an order a screen wants and doesn't hold yet is read with no version, so the first send at a free place shows what was sent at once; off a shift a taker's phone asks its own rounds whole, and their answer ends the off-shift card (the frame is also read again when the page comes back). One accent on the floor (no takeaway action off a shift or viewing, a secondary one beside "הוספת מקומות"), the picker's rows disabled while viewing, a refused line's own mark (`DraftLineRow mark`), serve buttons named by place and department, the desktop's list with the new takeaway and the drafts' counts, the mode's sync from the moment it opens (the menu screen too). e2e: Yoav's own hours only (his seeded month is payroll's), Shiran on a shift for the double send, signed out before the owner's door, the ready toast and tile heard by the poll, serve with undo and a new ticket served, the kitchen's struck line and the held block, the owner choosing on `/manage/order_taking`.
- After the second review (database, e2e, logic, security; each finding confirmed by a skeptic; every client fix holds for a screen hidden in an `<Activity>` and shown again as well as for a remount):
  - Words: the restaurant's and the café's `app.orders.mine.columns.place` ("שולחן"), in the migration and in the twin's `restaurantWording`.
  - e2e: the phone's switch between the items and the order is a Segmented, so `showItems`/`showOrder` and the two design walks press buttons (aria-pressed), not radios.
  - `/manage/order_taking`: who takes orders is a `PageSection` (h2) as the positions and the shifts are, so the page goes h1 → h2 (axe's heading-order) and the console's module h2 → h2.
  - Sends and the tab: the store is read once for each person and business and written as it changes, hidden or not (`change`), so showing the mode again never brings back an older copy; each round has at most one send trying it (`delivering`), and any with none goes again when the mode is on screen again, the page visible or the phone online; a hidden mode starts no new try.
  - The draft: `DraftStore.update` changes it from what it holds when the change lands (`storeDraftChanged`): the undos, a tap and the draft's own changes; a line on its way never comes back into it.
  - Leaving a screen: a take-back sent then (its time, the page hidden, the screen left) takes its "ביטול" off the mode's toast (`say` returns its withdrawal); a send's answer that comes after its screen was left says the toast and moves nobody; what was added to a new takeaway while its first round was on its way is that order's draft once it lands.
  - The clock and the news: the mode's clock never goes back to an older server time (`laterServerTime`); the ready news leaves out tickets marked ready on this phone (`readyNews markedHere`, until put back here); an order is held with the version it was read at (a place's page: the floor's) and the sync asks from the oldest, so one changed while its screen was left is read again.
  - Shared tablets: signing out and locking a station clear every `orders:` entry of the tab first (`clearOrdersStorage`, app/(app)/orders/orders-storage.ts), and nothing is written after; every access is in a try.
- After the re-review of those fixes (each finding confirmed):
  - A new takeaway's first round: its send waits while that round is on its way, whichever send it was (`sendWaits`), so a screen shown again, mounted afresh or reloaded opens no second takeaway; a round that went again with no screen waiting says "נשלחה" with "פתיחה" once it lands (never over a way back), and the screen that showed it on its way goes to its order (`sender.landed`); a send answered after its screen was left gets "פתיחה" too.
  - The tab's entry has one writer, the mode that read it last (`claim`/`keep` in orders-storage.ts): one Next dropped from its cache settles a late answer in its own memory only.
  - A ticket put back here stays out of the ready news until a sync started after the put-back answered has come (`readyMarks`, ready-marks.ts), so a slower sync from before it says nothing.
  - The mode's toast is a pure controller now (`modeToast`, mode-toast.ts): a withdrawal takes away its own toast alone and tells the gate at once, an older toast seen going after a newer one was said lets no news out, and the tests hold it (a newer serve's "ביטול" kept, the withdrawn take-back's write unheard); the news-gate test that only repeated the gate's path is gone.
- After the re-review of a898aab (each finding confirmed):
  - A send answered after its screen was left says "נשלחה" through the news gate (`tell` on the mode's value), so it never takes the place of a take-back's or a serve's "ביטול" on the screen now shown.
  - A withdrawn toast lets the news out only once what withdrew it is done (a microtask): the next take-back's toast, said in the same turn, keeps the ready news waiting instead of hiding it.
  - The gate keeps what waits by what it is about ("ready", `sent:<round>`) and tells each in turn, the next once the toast before it has gone, so a round that landed and the ready news no longer drop each other.
  - A ticket marked ready here is news again once a read made after the mark sees it not ready (`readyMarks.seen`): put back and made ready again on another phone, its owner is told.
- After the re-review of 8731add (each finding confirmed): a serve's "ביטול" is no ready news (`readyNews` leaves out a ticket served before and ready again with the time it was ready at; one served new and marked ready since still is); a toast whose time is up is forgotten (`modeToast.hidden` puts null), and a `StatusToast` drawn again with the same message (its screen shown again from a hidden `<Activity>`) goes on with the time it had left and never takes the focus again, so no finished "מוכן" or stale "ביטול" comes back on returning to the mode; the send's "נשלחה" on its own screen goes through the news gate too, so a take-back's or a serve's "ביטול" said while the round was on its way stays until it goes.

## 2026-11-20 · chat · taking orders: the orders in the database, module off (waiter mode P3)

- On chat/order-orders-db from main (879f5db). Orel decided on 20 November to build every phase, with decisions 3, 4, 5 and 7 as the design recommends (#1, #191). P3 is the database half of §14 P3: no new screen.

- After the review (database, security, tests and fit; each finding confirmed by a skeptic): `order_tickets` and `order_lines` get an index on `business_id`, so a demo reset no longer reads them whole (pgTAP: every foreign key of the orders leads an index); every write checks that each row it locks is still there and is refused P0002 if not, never going on with empty rows (the send too, after its business lock); `cancel_order_line` asks who before any lock, and whether the ticket is ready after; `order_detail` and `order_till` refuse an order of another business word for word as one that doesn't exist; the job makes an erased order's rounds' body hashes from their ids alone (a takeaway's whose label was cleared before too), so no erased note or name can be confirmed by hashing a guess, and forgets after 30 days who opened, moved, reopened, closed or cancelled an order, called, made ready or served a ticket, and took back, cancelled or gave a line on the house; new pgTAP for a retry after the sender clocked out, the most ordered, a served ticket not put back to new, a view as a manager reading another day's order, and another business's place in a send and a move. `supabase/README.md` lists the seventh job.
- After the second review (database and security, each finding confirmed by a skeptic; the team's runner powers through `runs_orders` are settled on P2's branch, so P3 keeps calling it):
  - The job's note and hash steps touch only rounds sent more than two days before its moment, and any order it marks that has a round from the last two days loses its mark again: an open one that got a round while the job waited on its lock, or one closed or cancelled at a moment before its last round's (the close waited on the send's lock, or the server's clocks are a little apart). That round keeps its notes for the kitchen and its retry, and they go once its own two days pass (pgTAP, two new cases in one transaction, every moment passed to the functions: an order closed a minute before its last round was sent loses its first round's note and hash and keeps the last one's, unmarked, half a minute before that round's two days pass, and an hour later loses them and is marked; 25). The open order's race needs two sessions, so no pgTAP case shows it.
  - The job marks each order once its two days pass (`notes_erased_at`), even with nothing to erase, so an hour reads only the orders not marked yet through a new partial `orders_unerased_idx` (in place of `orders_labelled_idx`, which nothing reads now), the open ones as their own branch (`orders_open_idx`); `send_order_round` and `set_order_label` take the mark away from an open order, so a later round's notes and a later label go in turn (pgTAP: six new cases in `order_taking_retention.test.sql`, among them the later round keeping its note and hash an hour on, its order's last round not two days old, and its retry its answer).
  - `send_order_round` refuses someone who takes no orders (42501) before the business lock. On that path it first looks, with no lock, for their own committed round with the key, the business and the same body hash, and gives its answer when there is one (a sender moved out of the departments that take orders since, whose phone retries); any other key is refused alike, so nothing of anyone else's shows. Only the clock's part (`clock`, `stale_shift`) waits for the stored round's lookup under the lock (pgTAP: a kitchen worker with another's round key and its lines is refused 42501, not 23505 `key`, 89; a bar worker taken out of the bar gets their round's answer again and is refused 42501 for a new key, `order_taking_access.test.sql`, 58).
- Checked here: `pnpm check` (Biome, typegen, typecheck, unit tests). Not run here: pgTAP, `pnpm test:db`, `pnpm db:types`, `supabase db lint`, Playwright: first in CI; nor the demo walkthrough below.
- The demo walkthrough of §14 P3, not run here (no Docker): for the next session, after `pnpm db:start`, in psql as postgres against the local seed. It rolls back. Each comment says what the plan expects, not what was seen.

  ```sql
  begin;

  create function pg_temp.b() returns uuid language sql as $$ select '00000000-0000-4000-b000-000000000001'::uuid $$;
  create function pg_temp.ori() returns uuid language sql as $$ select '00000000-0000-4000-a000-000000000001'::uuid $$;
  create function pg_temp.shiran() returns uuid language sql as $$ select '00000000-0000-4000-a000-000000000002'::uuid $$;
  create function pg_temp.noa() returns uuid language sql as $$ select '00000000-0000-4000-a000-000000000003'::uuid $$;
  create function pg_temp.yoav() returns uuid language sql as $$ select '00000000-0000-4000-a000-000000000004'::uuid $$;
  create function pg_temp.item(item_name text) returns uuid language sql as $$
    select i.id from public.menu_items i
    where i.business_id = pg_temp.b() and i.name = item_name and i.archived_at is null
  $$;
  create function pg_temp.place(place_name text) returns uuid language sql as $$
    select p.id from public.order_places p
    where p.business_id = pg_temp.b() and p.name = place_name and p.archived_at is null
  $$;
  create function pg_temp.refusal(call text) returns text language plpgsql as $$
  declare got_code text; got_hint text;
  begin
    execute call;
    return 'none';
  exception when others then
    get stacked diagnostics got_code = returned_sqlstate, got_hint = pg_exception_hint;
    return concat_ws(' ', got_code, nullif(got_hint, ''));
  end;
  $$;

  -- Taking orders on, as the team would switch it in the console; Shiran and Yoav on the clock.
  update public.business_modules set enabled = true
  where business_id = pg_temp.b() and module_key = 'order_taking';
  select private.order_taking_settings(pg_temp.b()) -> 'takerDepartments' as takers;  -- ["floor", "bar"]
  insert into public.time_entries (business_id, user_id, clock_in)
  values (pg_temp.b(), pg_temp.yoav(), now() - interval '2 hours'),
         (pg_temp.b(), pg_temp.shiran(), now() - interval '3 hours');

  -- 1. A send split into the kitchen and the bar.
  select public.send_order_round(pg_temp.yoav(), pg_temp.b(), 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001',
    jsonb_build_object('place', pg_temp.place('2')),
    jsonb_build_array(
      jsonb_build_object('item', pg_temp.item('חומוס'), 'quantity', 2, 'note', 'בלי שום', 'allergy', true),
      jsonb_build_object('item', pg_temp.item('בירה מהחבית'), 'quantity', 1)));
  select d.key as department, t.status, l.name, l.quantity, l.unit_agorot, l.allergy
  from public.order_lines l
  join public.order_tickets t on t.id = l.ticket_id
  join public.departments d on d.id = t.department_id
  where t.round_id = 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001'
  order by d.key;  -- bar: בירה מהחבית 1 × 2800; kitchen: חומוס 2 × 3200, allergy

  -- 2. The same key twice: the same answer, one round; another body: refused.
  select public.send_order_round(pg_temp.yoav(), pg_temp.b(), 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001',
    jsonb_build_object('place', pg_temp.place('2')),
    jsonb_build_array(
      jsonb_build_object('item', pg_temp.item('חומוס'), 'quantity', 2, 'note', 'בלי שום', 'allergy', true),
      jsonb_build_object('item', pg_temp.item('בירה מהחבית'), 'quantity', 1)));
  select count(*) as rounds from public.order_rounds where id = 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001';  -- 1
  select pg_temp.refusal(format(
    $q$select public.send_order_round(%L, %L, 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001',
       jsonb_build_object('place', %L::uuid), jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('item', %L::uuid, 'quantity', 3)))$q$,
    pg_temp.yoav(), pg_temp.b(), pg_temp.place('2'), pg_temp.item('חומוס')));  -- 23505 body

  -- 3. Yoav takes back the beer he sent to his own bar: refused, he cancels with a reason instead.
  select pg_temp.refusal(format('select public.take_back_order_line(%L, %L)', pg_temp.yoav(),
    (select l.id from public.order_lines l join public.order_tickets t on t.id = l.ticket_id
     where t.round_id = 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001' and l.name = 'בירה מהחבית')));  -- 42501 own_department

  -- 4. A taker's cancel, in the log with its price.
  select public.cancel_order_line(pg_temp.shiran(),
    (select l.id from public.order_lines l join public.order_tickets t on t.id = l.ticket_id
     where t.round_id = 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001' and l.name = 'בירה מהחבית'), 'guest');
  select a.details from public.audit_log a
  where a.business_id = pg_temp.b() and a.action = 'order.line.cancel'
  order by a.created_at desc limit 1;  -- name, quantity 1, unitAgorot 2800, where {"place": "2"}, reason guest, sender Yoav

  -- 5. A close settles the kitchen's new ticket as served; a late round is then refused as closed.
  select public.close_order(pg_temp.yoav(),
    (select r.order_id from public.order_rounds r where r.id = 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001'));
  select t.status, t.served_new from public.order_tickets t
  where t.round_id = 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001' order by t.status;  -- served, true (both)
  select pg_temp.refusal(format(
    $q$select public.send_order_round(%L, %L, 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002',
       jsonb_build_object('order', %L::uuid, 'place', %L::uuid),
       jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('item', %L::uuid, 'quantity', 1)))$q$,
    pg_temp.yoav(), pg_temp.b(),
    (select r.order_id from public.order_rounds r where r.id = 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001'),
    pg_temp.place('2'), pg_temp.item('מים')));  -- 55000 closed

  -- 6. Two first sends to table 3 twenty seconds apart join one order; a third two minutes on is told it's taken.
  select public.send_order_round(pg_temp.shiran(), pg_temp.b(), 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000003',
    jsonb_build_object('place', pg_temp.place('3')),
    jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('item', pg_temp.item('לימונדה'), 'quantity', 1)), now());
  select public.send_order_round(pg_temp.yoav(), pg_temp.b(), 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000004',
    jsonb_build_object('place', pg_temp.place('3')),
    jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('item', pg_temp.item('פוקאצ''ה'), 'quantity', 1)),
    now() + interval '20 seconds');
  select count(distinct r.order_id) as orders from public.order_rounds r
  where r.id in ('a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000003', 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000004');  -- 1
  select pg_temp.refusal(format(
    $q$select public.send_order_round(%L, %L, 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000005',
       jsonb_build_object('place', %L::uuid), jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('item', %L::uuid, 'quantity', 1)),
       now() + interval '2 minutes')$q$,
    pg_temp.ori(), pg_temp.b(), pg_temp.place('3'), pg_temp.item('מים')));  -- 23505 taken

  -- 7. Yoav clocked out 31 minutes ago: refused; 29 minutes after his clock-out he still could.
  update public.time_entries set clock_out = now() - interval '31 minutes'
  where business_id = pg_temp.b() and user_id = pg_temp.yoav() and clock_out is null;
  select pg_temp.refusal(format(
    $q$select public.send_order_round(%L, %L, 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000006',
       jsonb_build_object('place', %L::uuid), jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('item', %L::uuid, 'quantity', 1)))$q$,
    pg_temp.yoav(), pg_temp.b(), pg_temp.place('9'), pg_temp.item('מים')));  -- 42501 clock
  select pg_temp.refusal(format(
    $q$select public.send_order_round(%L, %L, 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000007',
       jsonb_build_object('place', %L::uuid), jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('item', %L::uuid, 'quantity', 1)),
       now() - interval '2 minutes')$q$,
    pg_temp.yoav(), pg_temp.b(), pg_temp.place('9'), pg_temp.item('מים')));  -- none

  -- 8. Noa, signed in: no direct read of the lines; her prep view has her kitchen's tickets and no price.
  select set_config('request.jwt.claims', json_build_object('sub', pg_temp.noa(), 'role', 'authenticated')::text, true);
  set local role authenticated;
  select pg_temp.refusal('select count(*) from public.order_lines');  -- 42501
  select public.order_prep(pg_temp.b()) -> 'tickets';  -- table 4's kitchen ticket and table 3's focaccia; no "unitAgorot"
  reset role;

  rollback;
  ```

  The items מים, לימונדה and פוקאצ'ה and tables 2, 3 and 9 come from P1's sample; the sample orders hold tables 4 and 7, which it leaves alone.
- Next: database-reviewer, security-reviewer and business-reviewer on the PR; then P4, the send and the kitchen's screens.
- Defaults applied (to ask Orel): a manager off a shift still fixes orders (only sending needs one); notes of an order nobody closed go two days after its last round; the log keeps a line's quantity and unit price, no sums; taker departments come from the template now, an editor with P4; businesses already on the restaurant and café templates get them too; a cancelled order stays cancelled; a takeaway doesn't move to a table; "seen" is someone of the department opening the kitchen's screen; a cancel and "on the house" are undone while the order is open, each logged.
- With P2 (#208) merged in: `20261120000500_order_taking_roles_hint.sql` writes the restaurant's and café's own sentence for the roles screen's hint again, with P3's base (taking orders, cancelling, "ready", on the house), only where the team hasn't changed P2's; the menu twin's copy of the restaurant's words follows (`tooling/template-wording.test.ts`). P3's `reset_demo_business` is P1's newest body plus the one seed line, checked by a diff.

## 2026-11-20 · chat · the clock's shift today, clear of midnight

- On chat/clock-last-minute, from main `05cd369`. `clock.spec.ts` "the clock knows the person's own shift today, and nobody else's" failed in CI on 17 November at 23:59:10 Israel time: its all-day shift (00:00–23:59) had just ended, so /clock said "אין לך עוד משמרת היום". Over midnight, today can also turn between putting the shift on and reading the screen. `shift-swaps.spec.ts` "a shift that has begun today offers no giving away" has the same all-day shift and that second risk (its screen lists shifts from today on); no other spec has one.
- `waitOutDemoMidnight(testInfo)` in `e2e/db.ts`: when midnight at the demo restaurant, on its own clock in the database, is closer than the test's own time and two minutes, it waits until midnight has passed and adds the wait to the test's time. Both tests call it before `scheduleDemoToday` and keep their all-day shift and their checks (the person's own hours, the manager with none and not Noa's; the begun shift with no giving away). Never a skip: near midnight only, a wait of at most about two and a half minutes.
- Not a shift around now: the swaps screen would still lose it over midnight, so it needed the wait anyway, and the all-day shift reads the same and takes the same path on every run.
- After review: every other caller of `scheduleDemoToday` reads its shift on a screen too, so over midnight it is yesterday's. `profile.spec.ts` "a person sets a photo from Today, and colleagues see it" (the manager reads who works today on /home tens of seconds on), `today.spec.ts` "everyone reads who works today" and `schedule-build.spec.ts` "a manager shares the published week, and its link opens that week" (Noa's shift today on the schedule tab, next week as next week) now wait out midnight at their start too. `scheduleDemoToday` throws in a test that has not called `waitOutDemoMidnight` (a `WeakSet` of its `TestInfo`, read through `test.info()`), so a new caller that forgets fails on every run, not only on the night it crosses midnight.
- Checked here: `pnpm check`. Not run: Playwright (no Docker here); CI runs the five tests at 390, 820 and 1440.
- Next: nothing from this. Defaults applied: none.

## 2026-11-20 · chat · CI: the local stack's port taken on a fresh runner

- On chat/ci-supabase-port from main (`05cd369`), under the night protocol (no questions). Nine jobs from 9 to 18 November failed at the stack's very first start, seconds after its images were pulled, on "failed to bind host port 0.0.0.0:54322/tcp: address already in use" (the database job three times, e2e six, five of them the phone job, on different runners and regions). #152's retry 15 seconds later failed the same way all four times it ran; every rerun on another runner passed. Not the stack's own containers: there were none before, and the CLI removes them on a failed start ("Stopping containers..." before its error). Nothing outside holds 54322 every time, so `supabase/config.toml` keeps its ports. Likeliest cause: 54320 to 54329 lie inside Linux's ephemeral range, so an outgoing connection (the pull's, most likely) can have 54322 as its own, open or for 60 seconds in TIME_WAIT.
- `.github/scripts/supabase-start.sh`, called by both jobs. `reserve`, a new step right after checkout, puts the config's ports in `net.ipv4.ip_local_reserved_ports`, so no later connection takes one. The start (`db start`, or `start -x …`) on a taken port prints `sudo ss -ltnp` and every socket on the port, `docker ps -a` and the networks, removes this project's containers and network, ends a docker-proxy left on the port, drops its TIME_WAIT sockets, waits up to 90 s until it is free, and tries again after 5, 10 and 20 s, four attempts in all, then `::error::`. Any other failure still fails at once.
- Checked: the script against stubs of pnpm, docker, ss, sysctl and sudo (taken twice then free, busy past 90 s, kong's port on the second attempt, not a port, taken every time), its parsing against the real log of run 44188825889, and `pnpm check`. Not run here: CI itself (no Docker).
- Next: CI shows the reserve step and a clean start; if a port is taken again, the printout says by whom.
- Defaults applied: the ports kept from outgoing connections in a step of their own, beyond the retry asked for.
